## 5.7.0

### @tallyui/core

#### Minor Changes

- 498c8f7: Adds the optional `TallyConnector.productEdit` surface with `decideProductEdit` and the `editProducts` cashier permission (ADR-095).
  No connector grants the permission yet.
  `ProductEditPricesChangedError` (a `CustomerServiceError` with `priceIds`) reports a write whose answer shows other prices than those sent.

### @tallyui/pos

#### Minor Changes

- 6815b01: `can` denies `editProducts` to a null or absent cashier (ADR-095).

### @tallyui/connector-medusa

#### Minor Changes

- 3a3fb75: The admin-user Medusa connector implements `productEdit` for a variant's base price in the till currency (ADR-095).
  An edit is saved only when the store's answer shows every other price as read and the edited price at the amount sent; otherwise it rejects with `ProductEditPricesChangedError`.

## 5.5.0

### @tallyui/core

#### Minor Changes

- 488a2d9: Catalogue now applies the Featured chip itself, as it does In Stock and On Sale, and draws it only for connectors that implement the new optional `getFeatured` product trait. WooCommerce implements it with product schema version 5, so a till resyncs its products once.
- a26b6d0: Adds the plumbing for a tax-exempt sale: `ServerCapabilities.taxExempt` and order.create's optional `taxExempt: true`, sent at the order's content version only to a store whose connector declares it. `precheckCommand` refuses it from a server without the capability and on order.update, and an exempt payload must carry no tax. No connector declares it yet, and the WooCommerce transport refuses an exempt sale. Nothing changes for any other sale.

  pos_orders moves to schema version 24 (identity migration).

- fcb0216: Refuse to reopen store orders recorded as tax exempt (`TAX_EXEMPT_REOPEN_REFUSED`), preserve rate codes when resending an exempt sale, and require `taxExemptCoupons` to take a coupon on an exempt sale (`TAX_EXEMPT_COUPONS`).
- 251ab2d: The WooCommerce connector can send a coupon-free tax-exempt sale to a stock, tax-exclusive WCPOS `1.10.N` (N ≥ 20) or `2.0.N` (N ≥ 5) store. The transport reads `/status` and `/site` and sends `is_vat_exempt: 'yes'` exactly when the store declares `taxExempt`, using the same gate with no option to wire. A failed read retries the batch (after sign-in if unauthorized); it never refuses the sale. `precheckCommand` now requires `taxExemptCoupons` as well as `taxExempt` for an exempt sale carrying coupons.

### @tallyui/components

#### Minor Changes

- ac8af99: Opening a category by its tile deals its products out of the tile, and going back gathers them into it, unless reduced motion is on.
- f3c5b80: Add 58 mm and 80 mm paper width presets to PrinterForm’s editable Characters per Line field.
- 488a2d9: Catalogue now applies the Featured chip itself, as it does In Stock and On Sale, and draws it only for connectors that implement the new optional `getFeatured` product trait. WooCommerce implements it with product schema version 5, so a till resyncs its products once.

### @tallyui/pos

#### Minor Changes

- a26b6d0: Adds the plumbing for a tax-exempt sale: `ServerCapabilities.taxExempt` and order.create's optional `taxExempt: true`, sent at the order's content version only to a store whose connector declares it. `precheckCommand` refuses it from a server without the capability and on order.update, and an exempt payload must carry no tax. No connector declares it yet, and the WooCommerce transport refuses an exempt sale. Nothing changes for any other sale.

  pos_orders moves to schema version 24 (identity migration).

- fcb0216: Refuse to reopen store orders recorded as tax exempt (`TAX_EXEMPT_REOPEN_REFUSED`), preserve rate codes when resending an exempt sale, and require `taxExemptCoupons` to take a coupon on an exempt sale (`TAX_EXEMPT_COUPONS`).
- baec438: Tax exempt math and sale state, behind the store's `taxExempt` capability. `useSale` gains `taxExempt`, `taxExemptAvailable` and `setTaxExempt(on)`. Exemption is order-level: every line, fee and shipping row keeps its net and carries zero tax, inclusive-priced items included, and the order total is the sum of those nets. It can be turned on only before a payment is captured, and turned off with payments taken (the amount due rises). It is refused while saving, with a payment in motion, on the receipt, on an after-close sale and with a carried coupon (TAX_EXEMPT_UNAVAILABLE, TAX_EXEMPT_PAYMENT_TAKEN, TAX_EXEMPT_LOCKED), and on a sale with a line priced in the other tax mode, where adding such a line to an exempt sale is refused too (TAX_EXEMPT_CONVERTED_LINE). Coupons are priced against the taxed sale. Parking keeps the exemption. A resume on a store that no longer accepts exemption restores the taxed sale and shows TAX_EXEMPT_REMOVED. Reopening a refused exempt sale keeps the exemption and its quoted figures: each line keeps its rate and each fee and shipping row its net, whatever the store's rates are now, and switching exemption off restores each charge's entered gross. On a store that now includes tax in prices, reopening drops the exemption and resume shows TAX_EXEMPT_REMOVED. Finalize refuses an exempt sale on a prices-include-tax store. Nothing changes for a sale that is not exempt.

#### Patch Changes

- 93b4b9c: Tell the cashier to finish the order in the store when it is tax exempt on the read after reopening.

### @tallyui/connector-woocommerce

#### Minor Changes

- 488a2d9: Catalogue now applies the Featured chip itself, as it does In Stock and On Sale, and draws it only for connectors that implement the new optional `getFeatured` product trait. WooCommerce implements it with product schema version 5, so a till resyncs its products once.
- 251ab2d: The WooCommerce connector can send a coupon-free tax-exempt sale to a stock, tax-exclusive WCPOS `1.10.N` (N ≥ 20) or `2.0.N` (N ≥ 5) store. The transport reads `/status` and `/site` and sends `is_vat_exempt: 'yes'` exactly when the store declares `taxExempt`, using the same gate with no option to wire. A failed read retries the batch (after sign-in if unauthorized); it never refuses the sale. `precheckCommand` now requires `taxExemptCoupons` as well as `taxExempt` for an exempt sale carrying coupons.
- 2e49b52: The WooCommerce command transport reads `/site` for coupon sales, `/status` and `/site` for cash rounding, and `/status` for split tender. The `acceptsCoupons`, `acceptsCashRounding` and `acceptsPaymentsList` options are deprecated: `false` still closes the corresponding gate, but `true` no longer opens it without store support. Removing the three options is a breaking change and will only happen in a major release.

#### Patch Changes

- a26b6d0: Adds the plumbing for a tax-exempt sale: `ServerCapabilities.taxExempt` and order.create's optional `taxExempt: true`, sent at the order's content version only to a store whose connector declares it. `precheckCommand` refuses it from a server without the capability and on order.update, and an exempt payload must carry no tax. No connector declares it yet, and the WooCommerce transport refuses an exempt sale. Nothing changes for any other sale.

  pos_orders moves to schema version 24 (identity migration).

- fcb0216: Refuse to reopen store orders recorded as tax exempt (`TAX_EXEMPT_REOPEN_REFUSED`), preserve rate codes when resending an exempt sale, and require `taxExemptCoupons` to take a coupon on an exempt sale (`TAX_EXEMPT_COUPONS`).

### @tallyui/connector-vendure

#### Minor Changes

- 573adbd: Read Vendure's `taxExempt` and `taxExemptCoupons` capabilities from `/tally/v1/info`.

### @tallyui/components

#### Minor Changes

- 286840c: `Catalogue` supports controlled `termPicks` and reports changes through `onTermPicksChange`; with `items`, term pills are drawn only when that callback is passed, and the app applies the picks.
- f9e82d9: `Catalogue` applies the On Sale chip itself: it keeps products whose price, or a variant's price, is a sale price in `currency`.
- 72c8f14: CustomersList and OrdersList draw v2's skeleton rows while their data first loads (`historyLoading` on OrdersList).
- ce68841: `useRegisterSession` returns `loading` while a bound register waits for its first session snapshot. `RegisterColumn` shows row skeletons instead of the open card while `loading` is true.

#### Patch Changes

- 10685ce: `Catalogue` reports the cleared search through `onQueryChange` when Enter selects a matching code and says "No products match the picked filters." when app-filtered `items` are empty but products are available.

## 5.3.0

### @tallyui/core

#### Minor Changes

- 9943567: ADR-048's rounding tolerance counts discounted lines on a store that taxes each discount as its own row. `TallyConnector` gains an optional `discountsAreTaxedRows`, and `needsAttention` takes a matching option, which `OrdersList` reads from the connector. When it is set, a `total_mismatch` bridge is a notice only up to ⌈(lines + fees + shipping + discounted lines) / 2⌉ minor units, where a discounted line is one the `order.create` payload sends with `discountMinor` above 0. Without it, nothing changes. `@tallyui/connector-vendure` sets `discountsAreTaxedRows: true`; an app that builds its own connector object must set it too. The vendurepos plugin must count its per-rate tolerance T from the same rows of the payload (vendurepos/app#532). The bound was probed on tax-exclusive lines of quantity 1; inclusive channels, quantity above 1, fees and shipping were not probed.

### @tallyui/components

#### Minor Changes

- 9a3f9fe: Product cart lines get the swipe strip with Remove only: drag or press the Total to open it, or fling the row off.
- 27f5f5d: A cart fee or shipping row now opens onto Edit and Remove with a leftwards drag, as WCPOS v2 does: let go past half the strip and it opens; drag past the remove point (60 % of the row, and at least 56 px past the strip) or fling it fast past the strip and the line is removed. Reduce motion snaps instead of animating. Pressing the Total still opens it.

#### Patch Changes

- 9943567: ADR-048's rounding tolerance counts discounted lines on a store that taxes each discount as its own row. `TallyConnector` gains an optional `discountsAreTaxedRows`, and `needsAttention` takes a matching option, which `OrdersList` reads from the connector. When it is set, a `total_mismatch` bridge is a notice only up to ⌈(lines + fees + shipping + discounted lines) / 2⌉ minor units, where a discounted line is one the `order.create` payload sends with `discountMinor` above 0. Without it, nothing changes. `@tallyui/connector-vendure` sets `discountsAreTaxedRows: true`; an app that builds its own connector object must set it too. The vendurepos plugin must count its per-rate tolerance T from the same rows of the payload (vendurepos/app#532). The bound was probed on tax-exclusive lines of quantity 1; inclusive channels, quantity above 1, fees and shipping were not probed.

### @tallyui/pos

#### Minor Changes

- 9943567: ADR-048's rounding tolerance counts discounted lines on a store that taxes each discount as its own row. `TallyConnector` gains an optional `discountsAreTaxedRows`, and `needsAttention` takes a matching option, which `OrdersList` reads from the connector. When it is set, a `total_mismatch` bridge is a notice only up to ⌈(lines + fees + shipping + discounted lines) / 2⌉ minor units, where a discounted line is one the `order.create` payload sends with `discountMinor` above 0. Without it, nothing changes. `@tallyui/connector-vendure` sets `discountsAreTaxedRows: true`; an app that builds its own connector object must set it too. The vendurepos plugin must count its per-rate tolerance T from the same rows of the payload (vendurepos/app#532). The bound was probed on tax-exclusive lines of quantity 1; inclusive channels, quantity above 1, fees and shipping were not probed.

### @tallyui/connector-vendure

#### Minor Changes

- 720b21d: The Vendure connector declares `discountsAreTaxedRows: true`, so the till's ADR-048 rounding tolerance counts discounted lines on Vendure stores (#1402). An app that builds its connector with `createVendureConnector` needs no change.

## 5.2.0

### @tallyui/core

#### Minor Changes

- 429e1ae: A fee or shipping charge entered in a mode other than the store's is sent in its own mode (`amountIncludesTax`), but only to a store whose connector declares `chargeTaxMode`. connector-vendure maps `/tally/v1/info`'s `chargeTaxMode: { v: 1 }`.

  This ends the one-cent drift of an inclusive fee in a tax-exclusive Vendure store. Without the capability, order.create sends the same bytes as before.

  - **Apps:** nothing to do beyond passing the connector's capabilities to `finalizeOrder`, as before. The WooCommerce and Medusa connectors need no change.
  - **Servers built on `@tallyui/core/server`:** a server whose connector declares the capability must pass `supported.chargeTaxMode: true` to `precheckCommand`. Otherwise it refuses every sale that carries `amountIncludesTax` with `invalid_payload`.
  - **Storage is one-way.** `pos_orders` moves to schema version 23, an identity migration: no stored order is rewritten, and v23 only adds the optional `entered` on fee and shipping items. Once a till has opened this version, an older build opens the store but shows none of its orders, as with versions 21 and 22. The sales it rings meanwhile are migrated in when the till opens at 23 again. Never roll an app back across this version.

- fd25afd: **Behaviour change.** Cancel order on store and Give back and cancel are offered only while the register session that took every till payment is still open, and the outbox checks again when the store answers. An app that wires `onCancelOnStore` (5.1.0) loses the button until it passes `OrdersList`'s `openSessionId` and `useOrderOutbox`'s `openSessionId`. An app with no register sessions loses it. Once that session has closed, a manager must settle the sale in the store's admin.

  A refused sale left part paid on the store can now offer “Give back and cancel” through `OrdersList`'s `onReverseOnStore`. The WooCommerce connector implements the optional `RefusedOrderSettlement.reverse`: it voids only the till's recorded manual payments, then reads the order again before cancelling. The outbox's `reverseOnStore` keeps the attempt, command ids and confirmed voids across retries. The till names each payment to give back by hand and leaves that money out of its expected figures; if a step fails, the row stays in Needs attention with the confirmed progress.

  The cancel notice of 5.1.0 was wrong. It asked for cash given back after Cancel order on store to be recorded as a pay-out, which counted that cash twice against the store's figures. It now lists what to give back, and the till's expected cash, drawer terms and closure leave out the payments of an order cancelled on the store and each payment the store confirmed voided.

- 7cff720: Refunds price from an order line's as-sold figures when the store sends them: `StoreOrderLine` gains optional `soldTotalMinor`, `soldTaxMinor` and `returnedQuantity`, which connector-medusa maps from MedusaPOS plugin contract 7. A connector may declare `lineTotalsShrinkOnReturn` (connector-medusa does); the refund form then refuses a partial refund of a line without sold figures and asks for the store plugin to be updated. New `useOptionalConnector()`. Other connectors are unchanged. The medusapos app must pass the store's own rejection message (money_drift) on to the cashier: reject the refund form's confirm with an Error carrying that message, or pass it as `error`, and the form shows it as written.

#### Patch Changes

- 474f0bd: A fee or shipping charge entered in the other tax mode now keeps the cashier's entry on the stored order whatever the store declares (except under WooCommerce rounding). The Z, reopen, the first print, the reprint and reports tax it as entered, so they match the order's tax again. Before this, a 100 inclusive fee at 19% beside a 50 item at 7% gave a Z tax of 20 against 19, and reopen re-totalled 154 to 155. Only a `chargeTaxMode` store receives the entry on the wire; every other store gets the same order.create bytes as before. Apps and store plugins have nothing to do. Orders already stored by 5.1.0 never kept the entry, so they keep their one-unit drift; they are not rewritten.
- 809d968: `needsAttention` no longer lists a sale for a `total_mismatch` whose rounding bridge is within ADR-048's tolerance: |`bridgeMinor`| at most ⌈(lines + fees + shipping) / 2⌉ minor units, counted once for the whole order. The row still shows the rounding notice in Recent. A larger bridge, a `total_mismatch` without a bridge, and every other warning still list the sale.

## 5.1.0

### @tallyui/core

#### Minor Changes

- b1962cf: `precheckCommand` now refuses an order.create payload that fails the shape check at every version, not only version 5 and later, as `invalid_payload` with the same messages. The same applies to an order.update's create part at any `createVersion`. A command the store has already applied still replays as `duplicate` when the plugin runs its replay lookup before `precheckCommand`, as documented.
- aba5fd7: A refused sale whose order the store created part paid can now have the rest recorded on the store from the till, with no new money taken. `@tallyui/core` adds `SettlePayment`, `SettlePayRequest` and `SettlePayResult`, and `RefusedOrderSettlement` gains an optional `pay`. The WooCommerce connector's `pay` reads the order, records the till's unrecorded payments under new ids only when the store's amount due is exactly their sum and the order is still open, then reads it again: only a completed order is `done`. `@tallyui/pos` adds `canPayRestOnStore`, `unrecordedLegs` and the outbox's `payRestOnStore`; `useOrderOutbox`'s `storeSettlement` option takes the connector's `pay` too and the hook returns `payRestOnStore(orderId)`. The attempt and its payment ids are stored as `storeSettlement` (kind `pay`) before the call, so a repeat sends the same ids; no schema change. `settledOnStore` now counts a `gone` settlement as settled only when the store had recorded nothing. `OrdersList` takes `onPayRestOnStore`, which adds "Pay the rest on store" behind a confirm dialog naming each payment to record; with it, a row with money recorded on the store has no Retry. `OrderSyncNotices` says where the pay stands, never with the store's code or message, and a cancelled sale with cash legs now asks the cashier to record any cash they gave back as a pay-out.
- 93fb2b9: Adds `TallyConnector.settleRefusedOrder` with the `SettleOutcome`, `SettleCancelRequest` and `RefusedOrderSettlement` types, and the WooCommerce `settleRefusedOrder.cancel`: it reads the order the store created before refusing a sale through a WCPOS 2.0 read push, and cancels it with the revision that read returned only when the store's record shows nothing paid and the order is still pending or open; otherwise it answers `changed`, `refused`, `gone` or `retry`.

#### Patch Changes

- 2ef5a3d: `precheckCommand` now also refuses a malformed order.refund, order.reopen or register command payload as `invalid_payload`, with the messages of `refundPayloadErrors`, `reopenPayloadErrors` and `registerPayloadErrors` (at most 10, joined with `; `). The version check still answers first. Plugins still run the shape check themselves before any lookup; this is a backstop for one that skips it.

### @tallyui/components

#### Minor Changes

- 977dd67: A refused sale whose order the store created with nothing paid can now be cancelled on the store from the till. `@tallyui/pos` adds `canCancelOnStore`, `settledOnStore` and the outbox's `cancelOnStore`, and `useOrderOutbox` takes a `storeSettlement` option (the connector's `cancel` for the open store) and returns `cancelOnStore(orderId)`. The till stores where the cancel stands on the order as `storeSettlement`: `pos_orders` goes to schema version 22, an identity migration that is one-way, like version 21 (an older build opens a version-22 store but shows none of its orders; the sales it rings meanwhile are migrated in when the till opens at 22 again). A settled row (cancelled or gone on the store) leaves `needsAttention` and the outbox's `rejected` count, and `requeue()` never resends a row with a settlement. `discard()` now keeps a refused sale whose order the store created and resolves to false: the till settles that order instead. `OrdersList` takes `onCancelOnStore`, which adds "Cancel order on store" behind a confirm dialog; with it, such a row has no Retry, except a stock refusal, whose Retry reads "Try the payment again". `OrderSyncNotices` says where the cancel stands, never with the store's code or message.
- f20b7d2: `Cart` takes `taxDisplay`: `'itemized'` (the default, one tax row per rate, as before) or `'single'`, one Total Tax row with the order's total tax, tagged Including or Excluding tax and drawn only when that tax is not 0, as WCPOS v2 does when the store shows its tax as one total. `taxLabel` now gets a second argument, `{ code, ratePpm }`, so a platform can name two rates with the same percentage apart by their tax code; a one-argument `taxLabel` keeps working and the default label is unchanged. Receipts, `CartTotal` and every order figure are unchanged.
- aba5fd7: A refused sale whose order the store created part paid can now have the rest recorded on the store from the till, with no new money taken. `@tallyui/core` adds `SettlePayment`, `SettlePayRequest` and `SettlePayResult`, and `RefusedOrderSettlement` gains an optional `pay`. The WooCommerce connector's `pay` reads the order, records the till's unrecorded payments under new ids only when the store's amount due is exactly their sum and the order is still open, then reads it again: only a completed order is `done`. `@tallyui/pos` adds `canPayRestOnStore`, `unrecordedLegs` and the outbox's `payRestOnStore`; `useOrderOutbox`'s `storeSettlement` option takes the connector's `pay` too and the hook returns `payRestOnStore(orderId)`. The attempt and its payment ids are stored as `storeSettlement` (kind `pay`) before the call, so a repeat sends the same ids; no schema change. `settledOnStore` now counts a `gone` settlement as settled only when the store had recorded nothing. `OrdersList` takes `onPayRestOnStore`, which adds "Pay the rest on store" behind a confirm dialog naming each payment to record; with it, a row with money recorded on the store has no Retry. `OrderSyncNotices` says where the pay stands, never with the store's code or message, and a cancelled sale with cash legs now asks the cashier to record any cash they gave back as a pay-out.
- 27a3830: A WooCommerce POS 2.0 sale the store refused after it had created the order now says so. The transport adds the order's `displayId` next to `orderId` in the refusal's `error.data`. The new `storeOrderLeft` in `@tallyui/pos` reads that order's reference and how much was recorded on it at the refusal. `OrderSyncNotices` shows it under the refusal sentence ("Order #1001 was left pending on the online store. Nothing was paid there.", or "left part paid" with the amount and the total), and uses the general refusal sentence in place of `invalid_payload`'s for such a sale. `needsAttention` keeps such a sale even when it was marked handled.

#### Patch Changes

- 1584891: Cash rounding (ADR-094): a closed cash sale's rounding now stays under one increment. A cash part pick rounds against the sale's earlier rounding, so two cash picks of 3.33 at a 0.05 increment take 3.35 and then 3.30, closing at 6.65 rather than 6.70; a pick that reaches the rounded due settles the sale. The zero-remainder write-off goes on the last cash leg, falling back to the last leg only when no cash leg counts.

  `roundedPickTarget` takes the sale's live entries as a new optional third argument; a call without it rounds the pick on its own, as in 5.0.0.

- 79ccd4d: `@tallyui/pos` adds the pure `writeOffLeg` helper, which `addTender` and the tender pane's 16-entry check both use for the leg a zero-remainder cash write-off goes on (the rule is in the cash-rounding entry above), so the two always agree (#1365). Cycles of voiding an earlier card and taking cash at zero after a pick can no longer write off one remainder after another and undercharge the sale.

### @tallyui/pos

#### Minor Changes

- 977dd67: A refused sale whose order the store created with nothing paid can now be cancelled on the store from the till. `@tallyui/pos` adds `canCancelOnStore`, `settledOnStore` and the outbox's `cancelOnStore`, and `useOrderOutbox` takes a `storeSettlement` option (the connector's `cancel` for the open store) and returns `cancelOnStore(orderId)`. The till stores where the cancel stands on the order as `storeSettlement`: `pos_orders` goes to schema version 22, an identity migration that is one-way, like version 21 (an older build opens a version-22 store but shows none of its orders; the sales it rings meanwhile are migrated in when the till opens at 22 again). A settled row (cancelled or gone on the store) leaves `needsAttention` and the outbox's `rejected` count, and `requeue()` never resends a row with a settlement. `discard()` now keeps a refused sale whose order the store created and resolves to false: the till settles that order instead. `OrdersList` takes `onCancelOnStore`, which adds "Cancel order on store" behind a confirm dialog; with it, such a row has no Retry, except a stock refusal, whose Retry reads "Try the payment again". `OrderSyncNotices` says where the cancel stands, never with the store's code or message.

The WooCommerce connector's `pay` reads the order, records the till's unrecorded payments under new ids only when the store's amount due is exactly their sum and the order is still open, then reads it again: only a completed order is `done`. `@tallyui/pos` adds `canPayRestOnStore`, `unrecordedLegs` and the outbox's `payRestOnStore`; `useOrderOutbox`'s `storeSettlement` option takes the connector's `pay` too and the hook returns `payRestOnStore(orderId)`. The attempt and its payment ids are stored as `storeSettlement` (kind `pay`) before the call, so a repeat sends the same ids; no schema change. `settledOnStore` now counts a `gone` settlement as settled only when the store had recorded nothing. `OrdersList` takes `onPayRestOnStore`, which adds "Pay the rest on store" behind a confirm dialog naming each payment to record; with it, a row with money recorded on the store has no Retry. `OrderSyncNotices` says where the pay stands, never with the store's code or message, and a cancelled sale with cash legs now asks the cashier to record any cash they gave back as a pay-out.
- 27a3830: A WooCommerce POS 2.0 sale the store refused after it had created the order now says so. The transport adds the order's `displayId` next to `orderId` in the refusal's `error.data`. The new `storeOrderLeft` in `@tallyui/pos` reads that order's reference and how much was recorded on it at the refusal. `OrderSyncNotices` shows it under the refusal sentence ("Order #1001 was left pending on the online store. Nothing was paid there.", or "left part paid" with the amount and the total), and uses the general refusal sentence in place of `invalid_payload`'s for such a sale. `needsAttention` keeps such a sale even when it was marked handled.

#### Patch Changes

- 1584891: Cash rounding (ADR-094): a closed cash sale's rounding now stays under one increment. A cash part pick rounds against the sale's earlier rounding, so two cash picks of 3.33 at a 0.05 increment take 3.35 and then 3.30, closing at 6.65 rather than 6.70; a pick that reaches the rounded due settles the sale. The zero-remainder write-off goes on the last cash leg, falling back to the last leg only when no cash leg counts.

  `roundedPickTarget` takes the sale's live entries as a new optional third argument; a call without it rounds the pick on its own, as in 5.0.0.

- 79ccd4d: `@tallyui/pos` adds the pure `writeOffLeg` helper, which `addTender` and the tender pane's 16-entry check both use for the leg a zero-remainder cash write-off goes on (the rule is in the cash-rounding entry above), so the two always agree (#1365). Cycles of voiding an earlier card and taking cash at zero after a pick can no longer write off one remainder after another and undercharge the sale.
- f34ab3c: On a WooCommerce store, a sale with a fee or shipping could be refused by the server because a charge's receipt row was one cent off the amount sent for it. The till now sends each fee and shipping charge at its receipt row on WooCommerce-rounded sales, and the WooCommerce order's body is unchanged, as it is priced from the stored charge; the stored sale and its totals are unchanged.
- f20e9ed: On a tax-inclusive WooCommerce store, a sale with a till discount and a coupon could reach WooCommerce without the till discount when the coupon took off more tax than the discount, so the order total in WooCommerce was higher than the sale. The till now sends each line's discount net of its tax before the coupon, and the WooCommerce order always carries the line discount.

### @tallyui/connector-woocommerce

#### Minor Changes

- aba5fd7: A refused sale whose order the store created part paid can now have the rest recorded on the store from the till, with no new money taken. `@tallyui/core` adds `SettlePayment`, `SettlePayRequest` and `SettlePayResult`, and `RefusedOrderSettlement` gains an optional `pay`. The attempt and its payment ids are stored as `storeSettlement` (kind `pay`) before the call, so a repeat sends the same ids; no schema change. `settledOnStore` now counts a `gone` settlement as settled only when the store had recorded nothing. `OrdersList` takes `onPayRestOnStore`, which adds "Pay the rest on store" behind a confirm dialog naming each payment to record; with it, a row with money recorded on the store has no Retry. `OrderSyncNotices` says where the pay stands, never with the store's code or message, and a cancelled sale with cash legs now asks the cashier to record any cash they gave back as a pay-out.
- 93fb2b9: Adds `TallyConnector.settleRefusedOrder` with the `SettleOutcome`, `SettleCancelRequest` and `RefusedOrderSettlement` types, and the WooCommerce `settleRefusedOrder.cancel`: it reads the order the store created before refusing a sale through a WCPOS 2.0 read push, and cancels it with the revision that read returned only when the store's record shows nothing paid and the order is still pending or open; otherwise it answers `changed`, `refused`, `gone` or `retry`.

- 27a3830: A WooCommerce POS 2.0 sale the store refused after it had created the order now says so. The transport adds the order's `displayId` next to `orderId` in the refusal's `error.data`. The new `storeOrderLeft` in `@tallyui/pos` reads that order's reference and how much was recorded on it at the refusal. `OrderSyncNotices` shows it under the refusal sentence ("Order #1001 was left pending on the online store. ## 5.0.0

### @tallyui/core

#### Minor Changes

- 501e189: A cash payment that settles a sale under its method's rounding records the difference in `Order.cashRoundings`, counted in the total and stored in `pos_orders` 21 (identity migration). A rounded sale needs a store whose connector declares the `cashRounding` capability on `ServerCapabilities`; without it the till does not round, finalize refuses an order with entries, and `precheckCommand` refuses `cashRoundings` as `invalid_payload`.
- bfab53c: Order payloads whose display figures do not add up (ADR-063 I1–I3) are now refused as invalid_payload.

  A store that takes this version refuses such a payload from any till, including one an older till still holds in its outbox. The check runs on version 3 and later payloads that carry `display` and `taxByRate` and pass the shape check; a version 3 or 4 payload that fails the shape check is not checked here. A command the store has already applied still replays as `duplicate`. The oldest `@tallyui/pos` proven to satisfy I1–I3 is 3.20.1: 200 seeded sales built by each of 3.20.1, 4.0.0, 4.1.0 and 4.2.0 pass the new check (both tax modes, line and order discounts, coupons, fees and shipping). 3.10.0 and 3.3.0 could not be run, and the other versions were not run. The run does not build return lines, reopened orders or charges with `amountIncludesTax`, and those versions have no cash rounding. On Woo with prices including tax it leaves out fees and shipping (#1341), and an order discount with a coupon (#1342): a check that predates this change can already refuse those sales.

- 86f3d40: `parseCommandResult` accepts a stock-only refund result: `totalMinor` 0, `byMethod` `{}`, `refunds` `[]` (ADR-080, #1330).
- 96f8789: `StoreOrderRefund` gains optional `lines` (`StoreOrderRefundLine`): the order lines a store refund paid back (#1324).
- d104073: Add the parked needs_admin command outcome while preserving transport retries.
  Keep a marker on pending refunds and expose RefundNeedsAdminNotice with Check again.
  Tell cashiers when stuck sales need a manager in the store admin.
- 8e15960: A store sale now counts on its paid day. `ListOrdersQuery` gains an optional `modifiedAfter` filter (WooCommerce `modified_after`), and the WooCommerce connector lists it. The new `salesRangeQuery` builds a sales report's read: with `modifiedAfter` it fetches orders changed since the range start and created before its end. `reportOrderFromStoreOrder` places a store order on `paidAt`, or on `placedAt` when the order has none. So a WooCommerce order created in a report's range but paid after it now counts on its paid day, as in WCPOS v2.

  Upgrading: build a report's read with `salesRangeQuery`. A report that still reads a WooCommerce store with `dateRange: { after, before }` now loses every order paid on a later day than it was created, because the created-day read fetches it and `salesFigures` drops it from that day.

- 837bb5f: `@tallyui/core/server` now installs in a store without react: `react` becomes an optional peer, and the built server entry imports no react, rxdb or rxjs. Core now exports the till's BigInt refund share, `refundableLines`, `priceRefund` and their types `RefundableOrderLine`, `EarlierRefund`, `RefundableLine`, `PricedRefundLine` and `PricedRefund`, with `roundRatio` and `RoundingMode`, from both its root and `./server`. `@tallyui/pos` re-exports the same functions under the same names. `SUPPORTED_REGISTER_VERSIONS` now lists 1, 2 and 3, the register versions the till builds.

  This is not a major change. No export is removed or renamed, and the `react` peer only loosens. `SUPPORTED_REGISTER_VERSIONS` is documented as the till's capability, and a server passes its own list to `precheckCommand`. A server that passed core's constant as its own list will now accept register versions 2 and 3.

- 710b62f: `StoreOrder.cashierId`, `storeId` and `registerId` (all optional) name the cashier, store and register the store records for an order, as `ListOrdersQuery` filters them. WooCommerce reads `_pos_user`, `_pos_store` (a WordPress id; 0 means none) and `_wcpos_register` (written only by WCPOS, so absent on orders TallyUI rings).
- 04c375d: `StoreOrder.createdVia` (optional) says where the store records the order was created, in the platform's own words; WooCommerce reads it from `created_via`. Absent when the store records none.
- e5c8503: `StoreOrder.needsPayment` (optional) says the store reports the order still owes money; WooCommerce reads it from `needs_payment`. Reports label a store order's unpaid remainder `unpaid` when it is true, so Taken no longer counts it; when the flag is false or absent the remainder stays `unknown` and counts toward Taken, as before.
- 1704c9d: `StoreOrder` gains an optional `paidAt`: when the store recorded the order as paid, as an ISO 8601 UTC instant. It is absent while the order is unpaid, or when the store records no paid date. The WooCommerce connector reads it from `date_paid_gmt` and never falls back to the site-local `date_paid`. A value that does not parse leaves no `paidAt`.
- eda38cf: `@tallyui/core` gains the optional, online, read-only `TallyConnector.listRefunds` member, its `StoreRefund`, `StoreRefundOrphan`, `ListRefundsQuery` and `StoreRefundPage` types, and `StoreRefundsUnsupportedError`, so refunds can be dated by when they were made (ADR-081 decision 7).

  `@tallyui/connector-woocommerce` implements `listRefunds` over WCPOS 2.0's `wcpos/v2/refunds` read, reading each refund's currency from its order and rejecting with `StoreRefundsUnsupportedError` below WCPOS 2.0.0.

#### Patch Changes

- 2ebf613: A till now lowers its perpetual refunds to the store's figure after its newest applied closure, once nothing of its own is unsent, and raises them only from the store's figures at its newest closure number.

### @tallyui/primitives

#### Patch Changes

- 322fc9a: The packed types now load the `className` augmentation for react-native (`ViewProps`, `TextProps` and others), so an app that installs from npm no longer gets TS2322 on `<HStack className>`.
  An app that declares its own `className?: string` is unaffected. An app that declares `ViewProps.dataSet`, or one of the same props with a different type, must match ours.

### @tallyui/components

#### Minor Changes

- f0fac39: Back opens a v2-style cancel-payment view when money was taken. It lists the cash to return (each cash payment's own amount after its change), what is voided and what is refunded. A payment on a store-covered reopened sale that cannot be voided shows a refund line, and the sale can only be parked.

  A correction to #1214's note: only a cash `addTender` by amount with nothing due is refused with `NOTHING_DUE`. A `setTender` or old-draft cash payment still records the amount handed over until finalize nets it.

- 501e189: A cash payment that settles a sale under its method's rounding records the difference in `Order.cashRoundings`, counted in the total and stored in `pos_orders` 21 (identity migration). A rounded sale needs a store whose connector declares the `cashRounding` capability on `ServerCapabilities`; without it the till does not round, finalize refuses an order with entries, and `precheckCommand` refuses `cashRoundings` as `invalid_payload`.
- 7ea0d85: Catalogue `tableRow.expand`: an expanded variable product's variant rows get v2's filter bar above them, with one select per option group of two or more values to narrow the rows, and a Collapse chevron that folds the row. The filters clear when the row collapses.
- 741611c: `afterVoid(leg, voided)` on `TenderLedger`, `TenderPane`, `Tender` and Cart's `tenderLedger`: runs after a void that `beforeVoid` approved, with `voidTender`'s result, so a host reverses a journalled void the till refused. A leg's `beforeVoid` now runs once across every mounted ledger and the cancel-payment view (#1271).
- d104073: Add the parked needs_admin command outcome while preserving transport retries.
  Keep a marker on pending refunds and expose RefundNeedsAdminNotice with Check again.
  Tell cashiers when stuck sales need a manager in the store admin.
- f0aaa2f: Add cashRoundingMinor to return an order's cash rounding with its final line refund, and cashIncrementMinor to show a notice when a cash refund cannot be paid exactly.
- fecef2c: SettingsLayout takes navOpen and onNavOpenChange, and a navSlot function receives closeNav so a pick closes the stacked menu.
- ed0458d: TenderLedger, Cart's tenderLedger, TenderPane and Tender take beforeVoid, a host hook that runs before Cancel payment voids a leg.

#### Patch Changes

- e9b7baf: A taken tender leg is kept until voided: `setTender`, `startTender` and Back keep it, `removeTender` refuses it with `PAYMENT_TAKEN`, and `newSale` refuses an unfinished sale holding a captured one. The tender pane voids a till leg the store refuses instead of removing it.
- 6c7838f: On web, hovering a cart line's Total peeks the row 16 px left on v2's bounce, hinting that the Total opens the strip; hovering out brings it home. Reduced motion, an open strip and a leaving row do not peek.
- a502b9d: A cash payment taken through `addTender` records its own change, so `Order.paidMinor` and `changeDueMinor` no longer include change after a cash overpay; a cash payment with nothing due is refused.
- 469e97a: On a store with cash rounding, a cash payment for picked items now rounds its own amount to the cash step and records its own rounding entry, so the change given matches the cash actually taken (for example, items of 3.33 paid with 5.00 take 3.35 and give 1.65 change).
  A later cash payment for the rest rounds only the rest. A card payment for picked items is never rounded, and picked items that round to nothing in cash are refused, with a message.

  While another payment is in progress, a cash payment for picked items must be a whole number of cash steps (`CASH_WAITS_FOR_PAYMENT`). A sale takes at most 15 rounded cash payments for picked items, so that the order the store receives stays within its 16 rounding entries (`CASH_PICK_LIMIT`, `CASH_PICK_ENTRIES_MAX`). Once the sale has 16 entries, a cash payment that would add another is refused, and the rest is taken by another method (`CASH_SETTLE_LIMIT`).

  New pure helpers: `roundedPickTarget` and `remainderEntriesMinor`.

- fe90c94: The sale rounds once in cash: a cash leg that settles after a void, or after a reopen with new lines, rounds the sale's unrounded remainder.
  So the customer pays the one rounded total.

  **Breaking signature change for outside callers:** `roundedDue` and `tenderRounding` take the sale's live rounding entries as a new last parameter; a caller that leaves it out rounds a sale that already has live entries a second time. `voidTender` now refuses to void a payment carrying a rounding entry while a later live cash payment carries one (`VOID_LATER_CASH_FIRST`; the pure rule is the new `cashVoidRefusal`).

  In the components, Cancel and void now voids the taken payments latest first, and a void the core would refuse never reaches `beforeVoid`.

- ffe94bd: A store whose only method is rounded cash is told to void its latest rounded cash payment (or park the sale) at the 16-entry settle limit, instead of to use another method.
- 75678a0: With a payment in progress, a cash leg that is not a whole number of cash steps is refused with `CASH_WAITS_FOR_PAYMENT` (ADR-094), instead of recording an amount no coins can make; the tender pane disables Take with the same message.
- 8db58a3: The tender pane shows a change amount only when Take is enabled. Before, a payment still in motion for the whole balance left Take disabled, yet a cash entry showed a large green "Change" for the full amount.
- 0f77b6c: The customer email error is read from the `customers.invalid_email` catalogue key, so apps can translate it.
- 43520ae: The customer search and save error texts are read from the `customers.error_*` catalogue keys, so apps can translate them.
- 322fc9a: The packed types now load the `className` augmentation for react-native (`ViewProps`, `TextProps` and others), so an app that installs from npm no longer gets TS2322 on `<HStack className>`.
  An app that declares its own `className?: string` is unaffected. An app that declares `ViewProps.dataSet`, or one of the same props with a different type, must match ours.
- b5c6059: `OpenSaleTabs`: the + cell widens from 48 to 112 pt and the "New" label fades in when the blank cart becomes current, and narrows and fades back out when it stops being current, as in WCPOS v2. Reduced or unknown motion jumps straight to the end state.
- db82f0d: `@tallyui/components`, `@tallyui/pos` and `@tallyui/storage-sqlite` no longer ship their `*.test-d.ts` type-test files, matching `@tallyui/core` and `@tallyui/primitives`.
- 585bb79: The sales report and the Z carry a sale's cash rounding (ADR-094) as its own figure, so net, tax and rounding add up to gross. `SalesTotals` gains a required `roundingMinor`, and `netMinor` is now gross less tax less rounding, the pre-tax amount the product rows add up to; `ReportOrder` gains an optional `roundingMinor`, set by `reportOrderFromPosOrder`. `writeClosure` records a session's rounding as `breakdowns.cash_rounding_minor` (only when non-zero; `closures` stays at schema version 0), and `buildClosureDocument` shows it as `closure.cash_rounding` (`name`, `amount`, `amount_display`) with `has_cash_rounding`, labelled by the new `cash_rounding` label key ("Rounding"). The Taxes card and the taxes panel show a Rounding line when the period has one.
- b865f35: `useSale` returns `tenderHeld(paymentId)`, true when `setTender` keeps that payment. The tender pane offers the in-place terminal reference only for a payment `setTender` would replace, so a captured card payment a resumed draft kept with no `takenAt` no longer gets the input, and typing in it no longer records the card payment a second time.
- a353d7c: The Tile Size, Tile Fields and Columns headings in the catalogue and cart settings forms now carry `aria-level` 4, so they sit below a host card's title instead of rendering as an h1.
- 5eb584c: `TabsList` lays its triggers out in a row by default, as on the web; pass `flex-col` to stack them.
- 6fd93ca: Take is disabled while legs in motion cover the balance, and a stale NOTHING_DUE clears when a leg's status changes.
- 9c6ad4b: Tender's part payment and "can't give change" hints measure what is still due after payments in progress, as Take does (#1327).
- bcbb929: Tender no longer shows the "Part payment · … left after this" hint while Take is disabled (a payment in progress already covers the balance).
- 1fc5e66: A cash part payment that no cash can make is refused. The till offers the nearest payable amounts.
- 8ab788a: The tender pane no longer shows a rounded cash due on a store whose connector does not declare cashRounding; it shows and takes the exact due.

### @tallyui/storage-sqlite

#### Patch Changes

- db82f0d: `@tallyui/components`, `@tallyui/pos` and `@tallyui/storage-sqlite` no longer ship their `*.test-d.ts` type-test files, matching `@tallyui/core` and `@tallyui/primitives`.

### @tallyui/pos

#### Major Changes

- fe90c94: The sale rounds once in cash: a cash leg that settles after a void, or after a reopen with new lines, rounds the sale's unrounded remainder.
  So the customer pays the one rounded total.

  **Breaking signature change for outside callers:** `roundedDue` and `tenderRounding` take the sale's live rounding entries as a new last parameter; a caller that leaves it out rounds a sale that already has live entries a second time. `voidTender` now refuses to void a payment carrying a rounding entry while a later live cash payment carries one (`VOID_LATER_CASH_FIRST`; the pure rule is the new `cashVoidRefusal`).

  In the components, Cancel and void now voids the taken payments latest first, and a void the core would refuse never reaches `beforeVoid`.

- 6e28dd6: A payment leg is stamped with the session that took it, and counts only on that session's Z: a part-paid sale parked across a close, or a taken leg left in the cart by `cancelTender`, counts its cash in the session that took it. A pending or authorized leg is stamped when it is captured.

  **Breaking:** `useRegisterSession` now requires two options:

  - `drafts`: the app's `order_drafts` collection, or `null` if it never parks a sale.
  - `liveSale`: a getter returning `sale.registerView()` (`{ takenLegs, saving }`), or `null` if it has no live sale. The register is created before the sale, so read the sale through a ref:

  ```ts
  const saleRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof useSale> | null>(null)
  const register = useRegisterSession({ …, liveSale: () => saleRef.current?.registerView() ?? { takenLegs: [], saving: false } })
  const sale = useSale(…)
  saleRef.current = sale
  ```

  Why: without them, cash taken in a session but not yet on an order (a parked sale's, or one still in the cart) is missing from that session's Z.

  `useSale` gains `registerView()`, `writeClosure` gains an optional `liveLegs` (callers other than `useRegisterSession` must pass both `drafts` and `liveLegs`), and `takingsByMethod` gains an optional third argument, `extra`, for taken legs no order holds yet.

  `startCounting` and `closeSession` throw `RegisterSaleSavingError` while the getter says `saving`: a completion is in flight or its failed save has not been cleared. Live figures omit its legs while saving, since the stored order may already count them.

  `useSale`'s `onSaleCompleted` is now bounded by `SAVE_TIMEOUT_MS` (60 s). After that the sale shows a failed save with Retry. `onSaleCompleted` must therefore be safe to call again with the same `posOrder` (same `posOrder.id`) while an earlier call is still running.

#### Minor Changes

- e9b7baf: A taken tender leg is kept until voided: `setTender`, `startTender` and Back keep it, `removeTender` refuses it with `PAYMENT_TAKEN`, and `newSale` refuses an unfinished sale holding a captured one. The tender pane voids a till leg the store refuses instead of removing it.
- a502b9d: A cash payment taken through `addTender` records its own change, so `Order.paidMinor` and `changeDueMinor` no longer include change after a cash overpay; a cash payment with nothing due is refused.
- 501e189: A cash payment that settles a sale under its method's rounding records the difference in `Order.cashRoundings`, counted in the total and stored in `pos_orders` 21 (identity migration). A rounded sale needs a store whose connector declares the `cashRounding` capability on `ServerCapabilities`; without it the till does not round, finalize refuses an order with entries, and `precheckCommand` refuses `cashRoundings` as `invalid_payload`.
- 9cd0f2f: closureFigures reads a closure's figures with one rule (a missing value is unknown, never 0), and medianMinor is exported.
- d4c0002: A resumed paid draft keeps its parked tax rates for WooCommerce lines, fees and shipping until no parked leg is still captured or in motion (`OrderBuilderOptions.heldRates`).
- d104073: Add the parked needs_admin command outcome while preserving transport retries.
  Keep a marker on pending refunds and expose RefundNeedsAdminNotice with Check again.
  Tell cashiers when stuck sales need a manager in the store admin.
- e3884f0: A parked or live sale's cash rounding now counts on the Z of the session that took its leg, and `registerView()` returns `cashRoundings`.
- f6f5323: A sale's `complete`, `park`, `resume` and `switchSale` refuse with the new `REGISTER_CLOSING` while its register's close is in flight, and `registerView().saving` is also true while a park or resume is in flight, so a close refuses then (#1275).
- 97d9671: `useStoreRefundsInRange` reads a store's refunds by the day they were made, sorted into counted, unscoped, other-currency and orphan refunds. `SalesTotals` gains `refundBasis` and `grossAfterRefundsMinor`, so a report subtracts one named refund figure.
- 585bb79: The sales report and the Z carry a sale's cash rounding (ADR-094) as its own figure, so net, tax and rounding add up to gross. `SalesTotals` gains a required `roundingMinor`, and `netMinor` is now gross less tax less rounding, the pre-tax amount the product rows add up to; `ReportOrder` gains an optional `roundingMinor`, set by `reportOrderFromPosOrder`. `writeClosure` records a session's rounding as `breakdowns.cash_rounding_minor` (only when non-zero; `closures` stays at schema version 0), and `buildClosureDocument` shows it as `closure.cash_rounding` (`name`, `amount`, `amount_display`) with `has_cash_rounding`, labelled by the new `cash_rounding` label key ("Rounding"). The Taxes card and the taxes panel show a Rounding line when the period has one.
- b865f35: `useSale` returns `tenderHeld(paymentId)`, true when `setTender` keeps that payment. The tender pane offers the in-place terminal reference only for a payment `setTender` would replace, so a captured card payment a resumed draft kept with no `takenAt` no longer gets the input, and typing in it no longer records the card payment a second time.
- 3cd4444: useSale: a removal taken for Undo is now voided by any customer change (setCustomer, setAddresses, setTaxIds) or a re-tax, instead of re-taxing the restored item; restore() returns RESTORE_STALE for it. Park, resume, a new sale and the receipt still void it.
- 8e15960: A store sale now counts on its paid day. `ListOrdersQuery` gains an optional `modifiedAfter` filter (WooCommerce `modified_after`), and the WooCommerce connector lists it. The new `salesRangeQuery` builds a sales report's read: with `modifiedAfter` it fetches orders changed since the range start and created before its end. `reportOrderFromStoreOrder` places a store order on `paidAt`, or on `placedAt` when the order has none. So a WooCommerce order created in a report's range but paid after it now counts on its paid day, as in WCPOS v2.

  Upgrading: build a report's read with `salesRangeQuery`. A report that still reads a WooCommerce store with `dateRange: { after, before }` now loses every order paid on a later day than it was created, because the created-day read fetches it and `salesFigures` drops it from that day.

#### Patch Changes

- 3132605: Back and a repeat startTender keep a pending or authorized payment leg, so a later capture is recorded.
- f0fac39: Back opens a v2-style cancel-payment view when money was taken. It lists the cash to return (each cash payment's own amount after its change), what is voided and what is refunded. A payment on a store-covered reopened sale that cannot be voided shows a refund line, and the sale can only be parked.

  A correction to #1214's note: only a cash `addTender` by amount with nothing due is refused with `NOTHING_DUE`. A `setTender` or old-draft cash payment still records the amount handed over until finalize nets it.

- 469e97a: On a store with cash rounding, a cash payment for picked items now rounds its own amount to the cash step and records its own rounding entry, so the change given matches the cash actually taken (for example, items of 3.33 paid with 5.00 take 3.35 and give 1.65 change).
  A later cash payment for the rest rounds only the rest. A card payment for picked items is never rounded, and picked items that round to nothing in cash are refused, with a message.

  While another payment is in progress, a cash payment for picked items must be a whole number of cash steps (`CASH_WAITS_FOR_PAYMENT`). A sale takes at most 15 rounded cash payments for picked items, so that the order the store receives stays within its 16 rounding entries (`CASH_PICK_LIMIT`, `CASH_PICK_ENTRIES_MAX`). Once the sale has 16 entries, a cash payment that would add another is refused, and the rest is taken by another method (`CASH_SETTLE_LIMIT`).

  New pure helpers: `roundedPickTarget` and `remainderEntriesMinor`.

- 75678a0: With a payment in progress, a cash leg that is not a whole number of cash steps is refused with `CASH_WAITS_FOR_PAYMENT` (ADR-094), instead of recording an amount no coins can make; the tender pane disables Take with the same message.
- c67f2b0: A paid draft with kept coupons stays priced when a WooCommerce tax rate changes between park and resume.
- cda1585: newSale refuses while a card payment is still in progress, with SALE_CARD_IN_PROGRESS, instead of dropping the leg.
- fd62b68: Only a cash method rounds its due (roundedDue, tenderRounding, tenderFigures); a non-cash method with a rounding increment keeps the exact due, as the leg it takes does.
- c283168: `useOrderOutbox`'s `record()` now resolves only once the outbox's pending count includes the order, so a screen that renders on resolve no longer shows one frame without its pending sale.
- db82f0d: `@tallyui/components`, `@tallyui/pos` and `@tallyui/storage-sqlite` no longer ship their `*.test-d.ts` type-test files, matching `@tallyui/core` and `@tallyui/primitives`.
- 2ebf613: A till now lowers its perpetual refunds to the store's figure after its newest applied closure, once nothing of its own is unsent, and raises them only from the store's figures at its newest closure number.
- 422a96b: A refund whose earlier answer was unknown stays pending when a resend is refused or unauthorized. This prevents a new attempt from sending a second refund for it, while resending the same refund still works.
- 1483634: Two sends of the same refund attempt at once (for example a double tap on Check again) now run one after the other. A lost answer can no longer let a new attempt send that refund a second time.
- 1c41466: A WooCommerce coupon replayed over a held paid draft's line re-prices at that line's parked tax rates, so the total and the coupon's discount tax do not move under taken money.
- aa37c6c: A reprinted cash-rounded sale shows its Rounding row.
- 01626c6: A resumed paid draft keeps its taken legs on Pay and Back instead of dropping them.
- dbbbc16: openStoreOrder, resume, switchSale and the idle settings reset refuse with SALE_PARTLY_PAID while the live sale holds a captured taken payment, as newSale does.
- 3b441fd: A resumed draft's captured payment with no takenAt (parked before 4.2) counts as taken: Back, removeTender and newSale keep it, and complete() stores it without inventing a takenAt.
- 837bb5f: `@tallyui/core/server` now installs in a store without react: `react` becomes an optional peer, and the built server entry imports no react, rxdb or rxjs. Core now exports the till's BigInt refund share, `refundableLines`, `priceRefund` and their types `RefundableOrderLine`, `EarlierRefund`, `RefundableLine`, `PricedRefundLine` and `PricedRefund`, with `roundRatio` and `RoundingMode`, from both its root and `./server`. `@tallyui/pos` re-exports the same functions under the same names. `SUPPORTED_REGISTER_VERSIONS` now lists 1, 2 and 3, the register versions the till builds.

  This is not a major change. No export is removed or renamed, and the `react` peer only loosens. `SUPPORTED_REGISTER_VERSIONS` is documented as the till's capability, and a server passes its own list to `precheckCommand`. A server that passed core's constant as its own list will now accept register versions 2 and 3.

- e5c8503: `StoreOrder.needsPayment` (optional) says the store reports the order still owes money; WooCommerce reads it from `needs_payment`. Reports label a store order's unpaid remainder `unpaid` when it is true, so Taken no longer counts it; when the flag is false or absent the remainder stays `unknown` and counts toward Taken, as before.
- 6fd93ca: Take is disabled while legs in motion cover the balance, and a stale NOTHING_DUE clears when a leg's status changes.
- 1fc5e66: A cash part payment that no cash can make is refused. The till offers the nearest payable amounts.
- 9d7ef3a: The WCPOS receipt template shows a cash-rounded sale's Rounding as a tax-free fee row.
- 5ac22b5: Keep distinct tax identities at the same rate separate in the Z tax table.

### @tallyui/connector-woocommerce

#### Minor Changes

- 8e15960: A store sale now counts on its paid day. `ListOrdersQuery` gains an optional `modifiedAfter` filter (WooCommerce `modified_after`), and the WooCommerce connector lists it. The new `salesRangeQuery` builds a sales report's read: with `modifiedAfter` it fetches orders changed since the range start and created before its end. `reportOrderFromStoreOrder` places a store order on `paidAt`, or on `placedAt` when the order has none. So a WooCommerce order created in a report's range but paid after it now counts on its paid day, as in WCPOS v2.

  Upgrading: build a report's read with `salesRangeQuery`. A report that still reads a WooCommerce store with `dateRange: { after, before }` now loses every order paid on a later day than it was created, because the created-day read fetches it and `salesFigures` drops it from that day.

- 710b62f: `StoreOrder.cashierId`, `storeId` and `registerId` (all optional) name the cashier, store and register the store records for an order, as `ListOrdersQuery` filters them. WooCommerce reads `_pos_user`, `_pos_store` (a WordPress id; 0 means none) and `_wcpos_register` (written only by WCPOS, so absent on orders TallyUI rings).
- 04c375d: `StoreOrder.createdVia` (optional) says where the store records the order was created, in the platform's own words; WooCommerce reads it from `created_via`. Absent when the store records none.
- e5c8503: `StoreOrder.needsPayment` (optional) says the store reports the order still owes money; WooCommerce reads it from `needs_payment`. Reports label a store order's unpaid remainder `unpaid` when it is true, so Taken no longer counts it; when the flag is false or absent the remainder stays `unknown` and counts toward Taken, as before.
- 1704c9d: `StoreOrder` gains an optional `paidAt`: when the store recorded the order as paid, as an ISO 8601 UTC instant. It is absent while the order is unpaid, or when the store records no paid date. The WooCommerce connector reads it from `date_paid_gmt` and never falls back to the site-local `date_paid`. A value that does not parse leaves no `paidAt`.
- eda38cf: `@tallyui/core` gains the optional, online, read-only `TallyConnector.listRefunds` member, its `StoreRefund`, `StoreRefundOrphan`, `ListRefundsQuery` and `StoreRefundPage` types, and `StoreRefundsUnsupportedError`, so refunds can be dated by when they were made (ADR-081 decision 7).

  `@tallyui/connector-woocommerce` implements `listRefunds` over WCPOS 2.0's `wcpos/v2/refunds` read, reading each refund's currency from its order and rejecting with `StoreRefundsUnsupportedError` below WCPOS 2.0.0.

- 0226b90: The WooCommerce connector carries cash rounding (ADR-094): each `cashRoundings` entry is sent as a tax-none `Rounding` fee line with its signed amount. Only the fork's `order_create_v5` stores declare `cashRounding`; stock stores do not, because nothing has been proven there. The app must pass `acceptsCashRounding` to the command transport from `ServerCapabilities.cashRounding`, as it passes `acceptsCoupons` from `coupons`. Without that option, or on a WCPOS 2.0 store, a rounded sale is refused at push with `cash_rounding_unsupported` and no request is made.

#### Patch Changes

- 9fcdb7b: A fork store that reports WooCommerce POS 2.0 or later no longer declares cash rounding, so the till no longer rounds a sale that the store would refuse. A fork store whose version cannot be read no longer declares it either.

### @tallyui/connector-medusa

#### Minor Changes

- dd1ca32: connector-medusa: customer display signalling client (`medusaDisplaySignalling`) for the medusapos plugin's `/tally/v1` display routes (ADR-085).
- 96f8789: Reads per-refund lines from the MedusaPOS plugin at contract 6; a failed plugin read leaves refunds without lines (#1324).

#### Patch Changes

- d264c18: connector-medusa: a header value `Headers` refuses (ctx auth, device key, display token) is now refused as `network` without sending and without the value in any error, and a body JSON cannot serialise is refused as `too_large`, which the pos retry does not re-send.

### @tallyui/connector-vendure

#### Patch Changes

- 1910512: `@tallyui/connector-vendure` `listOrders` now refuses every filter field (`status`, `customerId`, `dateRange` and the rest) with `UnsupportedOrderFilterError`, as the `ListOrdersQuery` contract says, instead of dropping it and returning the whole store. Paging and `search` are unchanged.

## 4.2.0

### @tallyui/database

#### Patch Changes

- @tallyui/core@4.2.0

### @tallyui/components

#### Minor Changes

- c93d08c: ChargeForm: `sku` adds WCPOS v2's "+ SKU" row to the custom item, and `onApply` carries the SKU.
- d49555b: PickStepper: WCPOS v2's per-unit pay-by-item stepper, the one Cart draws while it has a `picker`, is exported for apps that build their own cart on CartPanel and CartLine.
- 24abeb8: ProductTable takes `variationsButton`: a variable product's row shows v2's '\{count\} variations' button under the name, which expands its variants and never selects the row. Catalogue passes it as `tableRow.variationsButton`. Off by default.
- 90b8ce0: ReceiptEmailForm: WCPOS v2's receipt email form, the one ReceiptSheet draws, is exported for apps that draw the receipt inline; it shows "Email sent" or "Receipt email queued" itself after a send.
- 2c77798: The payments ledger shows each payment's time (`takenAt`) and `Paid in full · {time}` once the sale is paid, as WCPOS v2 does.
- cae5320: ProductVariantPicker: `VariantOption.dimmed` draws values that no variant takes as faded but pressable.

#### Patch Changes

- 02feb90: `ReceiptSheetEmail.onSend`'s doc comment says what the form shows after a send, instead of saying the form closes.
- f0a8c08: The receipt email form shows "Could not send the receipt." when `onSend` returns an empty string or fails with an empty message, instead of showing nothing.
- beb6a55: SyncStatus says "The online store didn't say which POS plugin version it runs." under the sending or retry line when that is why sales wait.

### @tallyui/storage-sqlite

#### Patch Changes

- @tallyui/core@4.2.0

### @tallyui/pos

#### Minor Changes

- 3ea4653: `createWebBluetoothSink({ device })` writes ESC/POS bytes to a BLE receipt printer over Web Bluetooth, through `createEscPosTransport`. The app supplies the `BluetoothDevice` from `navigator.bluetooth.requestDevice`, listing `BLUETOOTH_PRINT_SERVICES` in `optionalServices`, and decides when to disconnect; the sink connects if needed, finds the first known print service whose characteristic takes writes, and writes 20-byte chunks 20 ms apart, the last with response, then gives the printer 300 ms before resolving. No dependency is added.
- 2715c1a: `createWebUsbSink({ device })` writes ESC/POS bytes to a USB receipt printer over WebUSB, through `createEscPosTransport`. The app supplies the `USBDevice` from `navigator.usb.requestDevice` (which needs a user gesture) and keeps it between jobs; the sink opens it, selects configuration 1 if none is set, claims the first printer-class interface with a bulk OUT endpoint, or else the first interface with one, and writes in 4096-byte transfers; a stalled transfer tries to clear the endpoint's halt so the next job can print. No dependency is added.
- e546a27: `@tallyui/pos` exports `taxedLinesFor`, which gives each line and charge the tax mode its tax was rounded in before `taxLinesByRate` splits tax by rate.
- f3f85f7: A payment leg records `takenAt`, the instant the leg was captured, and keeps it through park, resume, reopen and the stored order. A leg added captured gets it at once; a pending or authorized leg gets it on capture; the pre-filled tender gets it at `complete()`. A value that is not an ISO instant is dropped, never the payment. `OrderBuilder.setPaymentStatus` takes an optional `takenAt`, set only on a move to captured. `pos_orders` moves to version 20 (identity migration, opened through `addPosOrderCollection`); like every pos_orders bump it is one-way.
- 61dbe03: `useStoreOrdersInRange` reads every store order in a range page by page, with WCPOS v2's download progress; nothing is stored.

#### Patch Changes

- ca99ab3: `createEscPosTransport` passes a sink's `PrintError` on unchanged, so a printer the WebUSB or Web Bluetooth sink cannot print to fails its job with code `unsupported` instead of `failed`.
- 99d6063: `useStoreOrdersInRange` ends a read with an error when a page says there is more but adds no new order and no unreadable one, so a store that keeps repeating a page stops, unless that page reports unreadable orders.
- faa5656: `useStoreOrdersInRange` stops a read whose `hasMore` page adds no new order even when that page reports unreadable orders, and counts unreadable orders only from the first page and from later pages that add a new order, so a store repeating such a page no longer pages forever.
  - @tallyui/core@4.2.0

### @tallyui/connector-woocommerce

#### Patch Changes

- 4985b75: The command transport now retries an unreadable `/site` with what the store answered: `status_404` when the WCPOS plugin is missing (so the till's "couldn't find the plugin" notice shows again), `status_<n>` for other errors, `network` or `timeout` with no answer. Only a 2xx without a readable `wcpos_version` stays `store_version_unknown`.
- 07babf0: A `/site` read answered 401, or 403 `rest_forbidden`, now returns `unauthorized`, as the register transport does, so a till that restarts with an expired token asks for sign-in instead of retrying forever.
  - @tallyui/core@4.2.0

## 4.0.0

### @tallyui/core

#### Major Changes

- c83dcda: **4.0.0: the till's customer-display API takes a device key.** This is a breaking change for apps that implement `DisplaySignalling` or call `createTillPairing` or `createTillDisplayService`.

  Migration:

  - `DisplaySignalling`'s nine till methods take `TillDevice { deviceId, deviceKey }` in place of `deviceId`. Implement the new `registerDevice`, and handle the `device_refused` refusal.
  - `createTillPairing` and `createTillDisplayService` take `device` (from `createTillDevice`) in place of `deviceId`.
  - Store the device key next to the device id, and never replicate it. An existing till registers on first use and keeps its paired displays.

#### Minor Changes

- fae1b1a: `@tallyui/core` adds `MoneyFormatProvider`, `useFormatMoney` and `useCurrencySymbol`. `@tallyui/pos`'s `CurrencyProvider` renders the provider, and every `@tallyui/components` money amount and money-input symbol now reads the store's money format when one is provided. Without a provider, nothing changes. `discountLabel` takes an optional formatter as its third argument.
- 028c0e3: `ProductCategory` takes an optional `imageUrl`, the category's picture for browse tiles.
- 3942708: `fnv1a32(text, { pad })`: the 32-bit FNV-1a hash the platform apps use for database names, as lowercase hex (inventory C22).
- 81198d2: `formatMoney(money, locale, format)` takes an optional store money format (`MoneyFormat`: currency position, thousand and decimal separators, decimals, symbol), as WooCommerce stores set them; without one it reads exactly as before, through Intl. `@tallyui/pos`'s `CurrencyProvider` now formats through it, and its `CurrencyFormat` type is `MoneyFormat`.
- fed2ec4: order.create version 10: a payment may carry `methodId`, the payment method's own id (shape-checked: 1–64, no NUL; refused below 10). New `wireMethodOf` and `legMethodId` helpers.
- 1f40158: `ratePpmFromPercent` is exported from `@tallyui/core` and `@tallyui/core/server` (moved from `@tallyui/pos`, which still re-exports it), so servers can convert rate percentages without vendoring pos.
- bc9a686: `reopenPayloadErrors` and `updatePayloadErrors` accept a `baseRevision` of up to 128 characters (ADR-090); a store revision is an opaque token, and WooCommerce's is 71. Ids stay at 64.
- c9403ce: `@tallyui/core/server` now ships a CommonJS build beside the ESM one, so CommonJS backend plugins can `require('@tallyui/core/server')`. The `./server` export map gains a `require` condition (`dist/server/index.cjs`, types `dist/server/index.d.cts`); `import` resolves exactly as before. The main entry and `./rxdb` stay ESM-only.
- b6e936c: **Breaking for DisplaySignalling implementers**

  - The nine till methods take `TillDevice { deviceId, deviceKey }` in place of `deviceId`, and `registerDevice` and the `device_refused` refusal are new.
  - `createTillPairing` and `createTillDisplayService` take `device` (from `createTillDevice`) in place of `deviceId`.
  - Migration: a plugin adds the register route and checks the key on every till route; an app stores the key next to the device id (never replicated); an existing till registers on first use and keeps its paired displays.

  A till now proves itself to the store with a key the store issued, so knowing a till's device id is no longer enough to act as that till on its customer displays.

- d034d9f: A store can report the lines whose figures it computed differently with the new `line_figures_mismatch` warning, which `parseCommandResult` and `knownWarnings` accept. `moneyDivergence(order)` gives a sale's differing order figures and line ids, and the orders list counts the differing lines.
- 82ec063: `ServerCapabilities.pluginContract` carries `/tally/v1/info`'s top-level `pluginContract`. The Medusa connector's `listOrders` sends register filters and 1-6 digit searches (display numbers) through the MedusaPOS plugin's `GET /tally/v1/orders` at plugin contract 3, reading the rows from the admin API so every order maps the same way; `medusaOrderFilters(capabilities)` gives the filter fields a store honours (#1114).
- e48cac6: `minorToMajor` is exported from the `@tallyui/core` root and `minorUnitDigits` from `@tallyui/core/server`, so each money converter has one name everywhere. `currencyDecimals`, `moneyToMajor` and `@tallyui/pos`'s `minorToDecimal` are deprecated in their favour; nothing is removed.
- ccb28e1: `normalizeMoneyText(text, format?)` reads a typed amount in the store's separators, and `useMoneyFormat()` returns the nearest MoneyFormatProvider's format (#672).
- 1821a2c: order.create version 11: version 10 plus the customer's `billing` / `shipping` on a sale with no `taxAddress`, as informational fields. `SUPPORTED_ORDER_CREATE_VERSIONS` and the version types gain 11; `precheckCommand` accepts untaxed addresses at 11 and still refuses them at 9 and 10.
- 74191f9: `@tallyui/core`: order.create version 12 is 11 plus `customer.taxIds`, a catalogue line's `sku` and order `meta`, as informational fields, with their shape and bound checks in `precheckCommand` and the `ORDER_TAX_IDS_MAX`, `ORDER_TAX_ID_TYPE_MAX`, `ORDER_TAX_ID_VALUE_MAX`, `ORDER_META_MAX`, `ORDER_META_KEY_MAX` and `ORDER_LINE_SKU_MAX` constants (ADR-092).

  `@tallyui/pos`: the till sends a sale's tax ids, meta and edited catalogue SKU at order.create 12 (`INFORMATIONAL_EXTRAS_ORDER_CREATE_VERSION`, now `ORDER_CREATE_MAX_VERSION`) only when the store's cap allows it, and `pos_orders` goes to schema 18 (identity migration, one-way like 17) to store them and `attentionHandledFor`. `markAttentionHandled(orders, orderId)` marks a refused or warned order handled, and `needsAttention` leaves it out until a requeue gives it a new command id.

- 0eb7640: order.create version 9 (#790): `OrderCreateCoupon.lines` (`OrderCreateCouponLine`) carries each coupon's share of each line. `SUPPORTED_ORDER_CREATE_VERSIONS` adds 9, `precheckCommand`, `payloadShapeErrors` and `payloadBoundErrors` check it (docs/contract/order-create-v9.md), and version 9 needs no `taxAddress`.
- f5a739f: `order.reopen` version 1 (ADR-090), an online command that reopens a store order for editing. `@tallyui/core` adds the envelope, payload and result types, `reopenPayloadErrors` (the strict shape check), the `order.reopen` type in `validateBatch`, an optional `orderReopen` list in `precheckCommand`, the `reopen` result in `parseCommandResult`, the refusal codes (`payment_in_motion`, `order_state`, `revision_conflict`, `forbidden`), `SUPPORTED_ORDER_REOPEN_VERSIONS` and the `orderReopen` capability from `/tally/v1/info`. Nothing sends it yet.
- 3b25108: `order.update` v1 (ADR-090): the contract for sending a reopened sale's whole new state. Types, a payload shape check (`updatePayloadErrors`), store ids on kept lines, fees and shipping (`storeLineId`) and on payments (`paymentId`), precheck, the `orderUpdate` capability and `SUPPORTED_ORDER_UPDATE_VERSIONS`. Nothing sends it yet.
- 7a6075b: `@tallyui/core` adds the `ProductTerm` type and an optional `getTerms` product trait, so a connector can report a product's terms (facet values, tags, brands) each in its group; without it a product has no terms.
  `@tallyui/pos` adds `productTerms`, `listTermGroups` (each group and term once, sorted by name like `listCategories`) and `hasTerm`.
- 2cb63cb: ServerCapabilities.receiptEmail: parseInfoCapabilities reads `/tally/v1/info`'s `receiptEmail: { v: n }` (a safe integer above 0); a malformed value is left out with one warning.
- 6e191c9: New exports the platform apps copied before: `deriveSessionFigures` and `deriveVariance` on `@tallyui/core`'s browser-safe main entry; `tenderLabel` and a new `syncStatusQuiet` predicate (true when `SyncStatus` would show only "Sales are up to date.") on `@tallyui/components`.
- 2bdae98: Adds an optional, online `closures` connector surface (ADR-093): read the store's Z closures, append a recount and record a reprint. `Correction` and `ApproverCredentials` move to core (pos and the Woo connector re-export them); `Correction` gains `late_refund`, which `deriveSettled` counts on the refund side. The Woo connector implements it for WCPOS 2.0.
- f51a692: A store order read now carries what a reopen needs to write the order back: the order's `revision`, an `id` on each fee, shipping line and payment record, and the recorded `taxStatus` on a fee or shipping line, a fee's `taxClass` and a shipping line's `methodId`. All new fields are optional. The WooCommerce connector fills them from the order resource and the WCPOS payment ledger.

  An order list page now leaves out an order the connector cannot read and counts it in `unreadable`, instead of failing the whole page. An order whose payment record the connector cannot read carries `paymentRecordUnreadable`.

- 28ba50e: StoreTaxRate gains optional `rules` for a backend's override-rate conditions.
- cc6f6cf: `StoreSettings` gains `preventOverselling` and `StockLevel` gains `backorders` (`no` | `notify` | `yes`). The WooCommerce connector reads the store's `prevent_overselling` and each product's and variation's `backorders`, which WCPOS v2's stock guard needs. The product schema goes to version 3, so a till resyncs its products once.
- 23a6813: `StoreSettings.moneyFormat` carries the store's own money format (core's `MoneyFormat`). The WooCommerce connector fills it from WCPOS's store row (currency position, thousand and decimal separators, decimals) and WooCommerce's currency symbol, so CAD reads `$`; pass it to `CurrencyProvider`'s `format`.

#### Patch Changes

- e928f11: A display's check code is unique among its till's live pending pairings, and the in-memory reference server now rerolls only against that till.
- ac46fa5: The `DisplaySignalling` docs now say the till's `deviceId` is a bearer secret on some backends (ADR-085, #753): an implementation never logs it, puts it in an error, a response or a display's view, or returns it to another device. The pairing and display service options say the same.
- 61846d3: The tender pane offers every manual-capture payment method the host passes, records each as its wire method plus its own `methodId`, and labels ledger, takings and report rows with the method's title; takings and report rows key by kind and id (`legMethodId`), so two methods never merge. `takingsByMethod` returns an object with no prototype; read it with `Object.hasOwn`, not `hasOwnProperty`.
- c9201a2: Internal tidy: `precheckCommand` builds its rejections through one helper, core uses `OrderCreatePayload` internally (the deprecated `OrderCreatePayloadV3` alias stays exported), and the order.create version docs now list version 6. Behaviour unchanged.
- a171cf5: `precheckCommand` refuses an order.create 9 payload that carries a customer address without `taxAddress` (#790).

  The order outbox treats an `unsupported_version` downgrade that would not go strictly lower, or would stay above the store's cap, as a terminal rejection instead of resending (#790).

### @tallyui/database

#### Minor Changes

- d1edf12: `measureCollectionBytes(collection, options?)` returns a collection's live document count and data size in bytes (UTF-8 JSON without RxDB metadata, not the bytes on disk), reading a page at a time; `signal` cancels it.
