- 2e98257: On a store whose prices exclude tax, a fee or shipping charge entered as including tax is sent at its 6-decimal net, so the store's tax and total equal the till's (before this, they could differ by 0.01 either way, depending on the rate).
- 81c579a: The closures and registers readers ask for their own route before the store list, so a store without them gets the empty answer.
- 3820381: Fix: a WooCommerce order read reports the payments the order actually holds. These are the captured rows of the WCPOS payment ledger, or, for an order without one, a single payment of the total only when the order is paid. An unpaid order no longer reads as fully paid.
- 316f607: The product pull accepts WCPOS 2.0.x's bare-array /variations answer as well as 1.10.x's sync envelope.

### @tallyui/connector-medusa

#### Minor Changes

- 28ba50e: Store settings list every tax region's rates as `allTaxRates`, with rate rules. Display only; the cart's rate is unchanged.
- 9dc9ce3: Order history for Medusa (ADR-081): `createMedusaAdminUserConnector()` now has `listOrders`, `getOrders` and `orderFilters` (`status`, `customerId`, `dateRange`), read from the admin API (`GET /admin/orders`). Money is converted exactly from Medusa's major-unit amounts, and a refunded order reads as sold, with its refunds beside it. New exports: `createMedusaOrders`, `toMedusaStoreOrder`, `MEDUSA_ORDER_FILTERS`.
- 870f16a: At MedusaPOS plugin contract 4, the Medusa connector's `listOrders` sends `allChannels=true` on the plugin orders route, so register filters and display-number searches read every sales channel, like the admin list. Contract 3 is unchanged and stays scoped to the till's channel (#1125). `MEDUSA_ALL_CHANNELS_PLUGIN_CONTRACT` names the contract.
- 82ec063: `ServerCapabilities.pluginContract` carries `/tally/v1/info`'s top-level `pluginContract`. The Medusa connector's `listOrders` sends register filters and 1-6 digit searches (display numbers) through the MedusaPOS plugin's `GET /tally/v1/orders` at plugin contract 3, reading the rows from the admin API so every order maps the same way; `medusaOrderFilters(capabilities)` gives the filter fields a store honours (#1114).

#### Patch Changes

- 1b9f3e0: Internal tidy: the WooCommerce order, customer and order-push code use `WCPOS_UUID_META_KEY` instead of spelling the meta key, and stale doc comments are corrected (the Woo coupon order.create version, Medusa's ignored `maxPages` advice, the errors Vendure's `gql` throws). Behaviour unchanged.
- 568ee6c: Internal tidy: the Admin API reads in the product and variant feeds and the id, price and stock reconcilers share one GET helper, and the price fingerprint builds its base-price entries in one place. Behaviour unchanged.

### @tallyui/connector-shopify

#### Minor Changes

- 7c813a3: Add `createShopifyConnector()`, a factory like the other connectors' that builds a fresh connector per call. The static `shopifyConnector` is built by it and is deprecated; it is not removed.

### @tallyui/connector-vendure

#### Minor Changes

- 791429d: `listVendureCustomers(context, options?)` lists a page of the store's customers with Vendure's list options passed through, the store's total, each customer's `createdAt` and its default billing and shipping addresses.
- ca32a01: Vendure products now carry their option groups: `getVariants` gives each variant's `options` keyed by group name (a cart line reads "Size: 32"), and the new `getVariantOptions` lists the groups and their values in the order the API returns them for the catalogue's option pickers. The products schema goes from version 2 to 3, so the products collection drops and resyncs once on upgrade (ADR-060 amendment 9); orders, register sessions and stock levels on the till are kept. Take the upgrade on a till while it is online, so the catalogue refills straight away.

#### Patch Changes

- 1b9f3e0: Internal tidy: the WooCommerce order, customer and order-push code use `WCPOS_UUID_META_KEY` instead of spelling the meta key, and stale doc comments are corrected (the Woo coupon order.create version, Medusa's ignored `maxPages` advice, the errors Vendure's `gql` throws). Behaviour unchanged.
- a1363ea: Internal tidy: the Vendure price fingerprint builds each variant's entry in one place, order history reuses its one tally-fields option, `REQUIRED_MULTI_INSTANCE_BY_ENGINE` keys on `WEB_STORAGE_ENGINE`, and stale planning and version comments are corrected. Behaviour unchanged.
- 7d90f84: A Vendure variant whose options all name groups the product does not have now gives `options: undefined`, as a product with no option groups does, instead of `{}`.

## 3.20.0

### @tallyui/core

#### Minor Changes

- 749894f: `StoreOrderPage` gains an optional `total`, every order the query matches, and the WooCommerce connector fills it from `X-WP-Total`, for v2's "Showing N of M" (#769).
- 5f3475b: `StoreSettings` gains `taxClasses`, each tax class's slug and display name, and the WooCommerce connector fills it from `taxes/classes`, so a renamed class shows its WooCommerce name (#770).

### @tallyui/components

#### Minor Changes

- e4fb919: `CartFoot`'s Checkout is at least 56 px tall (`min-h-14`) and its ⋮ is 44 × 44 px (`h-11 w-11`), WCPOS v2's sizes (#766).
- 8010117: `Cart` draws its totals and foot at WCPOS v2's inset (8 px in, 8 px apart), and `CartPanel` takes a `footerClassName` for its footer's wrapper (#765).
- 845b12f: `CartTotal` and `Cart` take `showTotal` (default `true`) so a v2-style cart can leave out the Total row, and the Total row is `font-semibold` instead of `font-bold`, as WCPOS v2 (#781).
- 378dcc6: `ProductTable` columns take `align` and `hideHeader`, heads are one line and 36 px tall, cells are padded `px-3 py-1.5`, and the default Price column is right-aligned, matching WCPOS v2's table (#768).
- 0b34330: Dialog, AlertDialog and NavDrawer backdrops paint the new per-theme `scrim` token (light 28%, dark 50% of a near-black, as WCPOS v2) instead of `bg-black/80` and `bg-black/50` (#779).
- 4244a8d: `SearchInput` is 44 px tall (`h-11`), WCPOS v2's search height, instead of sizing itself by padding (#767).
- 7896999: Switch's off track paints `bg-border` and Slider's track `bg-muted` with a `bg-card` thumb, WCPOS v2's tones, instead of `bg-input`, `bg-secondary` and `bg-background` (#780).

### @tallyui/pos

#### Minor Changes

- 2879bfd: `useStoreSettings`'s `ready` state gains `refresh()`, which re-reads the store's settings in place for v2's tax sync button, with `refreshing` and `refreshError` (#771).

#### Patch Changes

- 6b16ac6: order.create v4+ sends the right tax-exclusive line discount for a tax-inclusive line that has a till discount and a store-priced coupon; 3.19.0 took the tax after the coupon (#786).

### @tallyui/theme

#### Minor Changes

- 0b34330: Dialog, AlertDialog and NavDrawer backdrops paint the new per-theme `scrim` token (light 28%, dark 50% of a near-black, as WCPOS v2) instead of `bg-black/80` and `bg-black/50` (#779).

### @tallyui/connector-woocommerce

#### Minor Changes

- 749894f: `StoreOrderPage` gains an optional `total`, every order the query matches, and the WooCommerce connector fills it from `X-WP-Total`, for v2's "Showing N of M" (#769).
- 5f3475b: `StoreSettings` gains `taxClasses`, each tax class's slug and display name, and the WooCommerce connector fills it from `taxes/classes`, so a renamed class shows its WooCommerce name (#770).

## 3.19.0

### @tallyui/core

#### Minor Changes

- a3387fc: Customers carry an optional `createdAt`, and the optional connector method `searchCustomersPage` returns a page of a customer search with the store's total (`CustomerPage`); the WooCommerce connector fills both, from `date_created_gmt` and the `X-WP-Total` header (#680).
- 1af7329: The second-device customer display's signalling seam (ADR-085 U2, #684, #705). `TallyConnector.displaySignalling` is an optional `DisplaySignalling`: a till side (mint a code, list pending claims with their check codes, confirm or decline, list and forget displays, a signal mailbox, the snapshot post) under a cashier session holding `customer_display.pair` (`DISPLAY_PAIR_CAPABILITY`), and a display side (claim, collect the token once, its own mailbox, the snapshot read) gated by the display's token. Every refusal is a `DisplaySignallingError`. `ServerCapabilities.customerDisplay` carries `/tally/v1/info`'s display advertisement, read by `parseDisplayAdvertisement` with `DEFAULT_ICE_SERVERS`. `rateLimitSource` keys failed claims per source (IPv6 by /64). `@tallyui/core/server` adds `createMemoryDisplaySignalling`, an in-memory reference server, and `displaySignallingContract`, the shared suite each plugin's connector runs against its own routes through a `DisplaySignallingHarness`.
- 434c143: order.create version 8 taxes a sale on the customer's address (#722, step 2). Version 8 is version 7 plus `payload.taxAddress` (`'billing'` or `'shipping'`, the address the till matched the rates against) and the customer's `billing` and `shipping` addresses. A till sends 8 only when a sale was taxed on one of the customer's addresses, and never downgrades such a sale: capped below 8, `toOrderCreateEnvelope` throws `UnsupportedOrderVersionError(8, cap)` and the outbox sends it at 8, never at a version whose server would tax it on the base. A sale taxed on the base sends the same bytes as before. The server precheck refuses `taxAddress` or customer addresses below 8, and 8 without `taxAddress`.

  `@tallyui/pos`: `saleTaxContexts(settings, { orderCreateVersion })` re-matches rates by the customer's address only from order.create 8 (`TAX_ADDRESS_ORDER_CREATE_VERSION`); without the option, or below 8, every customer gets the store's context. `finalizeOrder` takes `taxLocation` and stores the tax address with the customer's addresses. `useSale` refuses a customer whose rates differ from the sale's once tender has begun (`CUSTOMER_RETAX_REFUSED`), telling the cashier to cancel the payment and change the customer in the cart, or start a new order. This is an interim: #759 re-taxes the sale on a customer change, as WCPOS v2 does. `pos_orders` moves to schema 12; its migration is the identity, and a schema-11 till opening a schema-12 store finds no orders.

  `@tallyui/connector-woocommerce`: `wooStoreSettings` fills `taxRateTable` whenever the store has a base address and advertises order.create 8 where it advertised 7. `toWooOrderPayload` sends a version-8 sale's tax address as WCPOS's `_woocommerce_pos_tax_based_on` meta, with the customer's billing and shipping addresses.

- a15835f: `ListOrdersQuery` takes v2's order-history filters (#721): `status`, `customerId`, `cashierId`, `registerId`, `paymentMethod`, `storeId`, `createdVia` and `dateRange` (`after` and `before` as ISO 8601 UTC instants ending in `Z`). A connector lists the fields its `listOrders` honours in the new optional `TallyConnector.orderFilters` and maps each its own way; any other set field is refused with `UnsupportedOrderFilterError` (`code: 'unsupported_order_filter'`, `fields`), which `assertOrderFiltersSupported` gives. Also exports `OrderFilterField`, `ORDER_FILTER_FIELDS`, `orderFilterFields` and `assertOrderDateRange`.
- f819768: `receiptTemplates()` lists the store's renderable print templates; WooCommerce reads them from the WCPOS templates route and labels them with the `wcpos` data model.
- 2e691f2: `OrderBuilder.setCoupons` takes a store-priced coupon context (`StorePricedCouponContext`): the store's per-line amounts come off the lines in any rounding mode, `Order.coupons` and `display.coupons` carry each code's share of the settlement totals, and `couponPricesStale` flags an order changed since the store priced it, which `finalizeOrder` refuses; `couponPricingBasis` gives what the prices depend on. `parseInfoCapabilities` reads `coupons: true` from `/tally/v1/info` (#709).
- 3a6eb07: `SyncContext` takes an optional `fetch`, resolved per request and defaulting to `globalThis.fetch`, so a host can pass a wrapper such as `meteredFetch` to count requests.
  The WooCommerce connector sends every pull, reconcile, settings, payment-methods, receipt-templates and customers request through `context.fetch` when it is set.
- 41aa864: WooCommerce's tax location, the pure pieces (#722). `woocommerceTax.orderTaxLocation(base, taxBasedOn, order?)` resolves the address an order is taxed by, as `WC_Abstract_Order::get_tax_location()` does: `shipping` falls back to billing and then to the shop base, `billing` to the shop base, and the order's `_woocommerce_pos_tax_based_on` (`TAX_BASED_ON_META_KEY`) overrides the store's basis when valid; `normalizeTaxBasedOn` turns anything else into `base`. `StoreSettings` gains three optional fields a connector fills: `taxBasedOn`, `baseTaxAddress` and `taxRateTable` (the unfiltered WooCommerce rate table). `taxSettingsForLocation(settings, location)` in `@tallyui/pos` re-derives `taxRates` and `taxRatesPpm` for an address from `taxRateTable` (a class's ppm includes compound rates; a class with no matching rate is 0), and returns the settings unchanged when there is no table. Nothing uses them yet.

### @tallyui/database

#### Minor Changes

- 98f7dfd: A local `request_metrics` collection keeps one row per hour, and `persistRequestMetrics` hydrates a request-metrics counter from it, writes the changed hours every 5 minutes and on `stop()`, and deletes the hours that fall out of the newest 48 (#683).
- 3e33d02: Hourly request metrics ported from WCPOS v2: `createRequestMetrics` keeps the newest 48 hourly buckets of requests, bytes, durations, failures and server load, and `meteredFetch` wraps a `fetch` so each request is recorded.
- 6adf1c5: Sync cadence presets (Eco, Balanced, Realtime, ported from WCPOS v2) and a device-local `sync_cadence` collection that holds the chosen profile, with `readSyncCadence`, `syncCadence$`, `setSyncCadence` and `resetSyncCadence`.
- b3f790b: `startSyncCadence` is an opt-in runner that calls `reSync()` on its targets on a jittered timer set by the sync cadence profile, and `startReplication` takes an optional `pullBatchSize` that overrides the adapter's pull page size (#683).

### @tallyui/primitives

#### Minor Changes

- e857cf2: `Tooltip.Trigger` now opens the way the docs describe (#749): hovering opens it after `Tooltip.Root`'s `delayDuration` (default `0`, now read), leaving closes it, keyboard focus opens it and blur closes it; the focus a mouse press causes does not open it. A press no longer opens a tooltip, so a tooltip-wrapped button such as `CameraScanButton` stops showing its tooltip on press; a press still closes an open tooltip and then calls the trigger's `onPress`. The new `openOnPress` prop (default `false`) brings back press-to-toggle for a trigger that does nothing else; on touch, where there is no hover, the tooltip opens only with `openOnPress`. The trigger's own `onHoverIn`, `onHoverOut`, `onFocus` and `onBlur` are still called and `onLongPress` passes through untouched, a pending hover delay is cancelled by leaving, by a press and by unmounting, and a disabled trigger opens on none of these.

### @tallyui/components

#### Minor Changes

- d6c93e9: A camera barcode scanner (#720, v2's scan button and inline camera panel). `Catalogue` takes `cameraScan` (default false) and `cameraScanner`; with both it puts a `CameraScanButton` beside the search box that opens a `CameraScannerPanel`. The panel calls no pos hook: the app owns `useCameraScan` (inside its `ScanHubProvider`) and passes `{ onDetected: camera.offer, onOpen: camera.reset, scanCount: camera.scanCount }`, so accepted decodes go to the scan hub as `{ source: { kind: 'camera' } }` and the app's `useBarcodeScanner` receives them like device scans. The panel shows v2's status copy, a permission card for native apps, a flash on each `scanCount` change, and a resize handle with increment and decrement actions and a web pointer drag, reporting the height through `onHeightChange`. On the web, `ScannerViewfinder` streams `getUserMedia` into a `<video>` and decodes with the `BarcodeDetector` global when present, or with an injected `decoder` of the same `detect` shape; native apps pass their own `viewfinder`. `@tallyui/pos` adds `useCameraScan` (returning `CameraScan`: `offer`, `reset`, `scanCount`), the pure `startDecodeLoop`, `nativeBarcodeDetector`, `cameraErrorStatus`, `detectorFormatToSymbology` and their constants, and `createScanSession` takes `continuous`, under which a refused repeat restarts its cooldown, so an item resting in view is scanned once.
- 44e9e5f: `Cart` takes `columns`, v2's Cart Settings › Columns (#695): each of Qty, Image, Item, SKU, Price and Total has a `show` switch, `quantity.split` adds a Split link under a quantity above 1, and `name.sku` puts the SKU under the name. The defaults keep today's cart (Qty, Item, Price folding as before, Total). `CartColumnHeads` and `CartLine` take the same `columns`, and `CartLine` takes `sku`, `imageUrl` and `onSplit`; `cartColumnIds` gives the visible columns. `OrderBuilder.splitLine` and `useSale().splitLine` turn a line of quantity N into N lines of 1, refusing a line with a fixed discount (`SPLIT_FIXED_DISCOUNT`). `cartColumnIds` and `CartColumnSettings` are exported from the `@tallyui/components` root.
- 44faba9: An empty `Cart` now shows v2's empty state: a centred cart icon over one line, and no column heads, totals, Cash and Card terminal row or Checkout foot. A sale error on an empty cart shows under the line. The default line changed from "Scan or tap a product to start a sale." to v2's "Tap a product to start a sale"; `emptyText` and `emptyIcon` (`null` for none) replace them. A cart with only a fee or shipping row keeps its heads and foot and shows no empty line. Adds `ShoppingCartIcon`.
- 259398e: In `lineEditor="popover"` mode the cart line is one row with Discount, Price and Remove in its popovers, and the price popover shows the regular price struck through; `LinePopover` takes `actions` and `regularValue`.
- d6d37ba: ChargeForm takes `kind` to open on one kind without the kind row, for apps that host WCPOS v2's cart "+" menu.
- f256a99: Add `Calendar` (single and range modes, `min`/`max`, a week start; a plain month grid with no new dependency) and the pure month helpers it uses, and `DateRangeMenu`, the Reports period menu with the date presets beside the calendar (WCPOS v2's date button). The Sales room's period control is now that menu, so a custom range can be picked; `SalesFiltersBar` takes an optional `earliestDay`. `SalesFilters.preset` widens from `DatePreset` to `DatePreset | 'custom'`, so code that reads it must handle `'custom'`. The separate `sales-filter-dates` text is gone: the menu's trigger shows the dates.
- 062e6a2: The second-device customer display's two screens (ADR-085 U5, #684). `CustomerDisplaySettings` is the till's Settings › Customer display: it takes `useTillPairing(pairing)` and `useTillDisplays(service)` and lists the paired displays with their state (Connected, Connecting…, Waiting for the display, Offline), forgets one after an `AlertDialog` confirm, mints a code with its expiry, and shows each "wants to pair" claim with its check code and Confirm and Decline (#705). Every pairing and forget refusal, and the offline and paused statuses, show a line. `CustomerDisplayPairing` is the display device's screen: it takes `useDisplayClient(client)`, enters the code and the display's name, shows the check code while the cashier confirms, then shows the sale through `CustomerDisplayScreen` with a small Offline notice when the store can't be reached. A forgotten display goes back to enter-code with its message.
- 61c02b9: Add `OrderFilterBar`, WCPOS v2's row of order filter pills (Status, Payment Method, Customer, Cashier, Store / Created via, Register, Date range) with Clear all. It is presentational: the app passes the value, the connector's `orderFilters` and every option list, and a pill shows only when its field is supported. pos adds `OrderFilterValue` and `orderFiltersQuery(value, timezone)`, which turns the bar's value into `ListOrdersQuery` filter fields, with the store-day range as UTC instants (`before` exclusive).
- e2be8d1: `RegisterPanel` takes `presentation="side"`, WCPOS v2's right-hand register sheet (#716): a header with the store, "open since", the session's age and the opener; the cash expected in large type "in the drawer"; Session sales with the sales count; the Paid in, Paid out and No sale tiles; the drawer ledger (Float, Cash sales, Cash refunds, Paid in, Paid out and No sale, each with its count, then Expected in the drawer); Other tenders; and a footer with Print X-report and Close register. Blind counting hides every figure. The new `storeName`, `onPrintXReport` and `now` props serve the side sheet; the default `presentation="dialog"` is unchanged. `DialogContent` takes `side="right"`. `deriveDrawerTerms` gives `deriveExpected`'s figures term by term with row counts and session sales, and `useRegisterSession` returns them as `terms`.
- 1c44032: `SelectTrigger` now draws a down chevron at its right by default, the v2 way (#717). An app that passes its own chevron as a child must either remove it or pass `chevron={false}`, or it will show two. `Avatar` takes `tone` (`'muted'`, the default and today's look, or `'primary'`, v2's light initials on a solid primary circle) (#718). `CustomerSelect` takes `showAvatar` (off by default) to draw an initials avatar before each row and the selected summary, and `avatarTone` (default `'primary'`) (#714). Adds `ChevronDownIcon` and `avatarInitials`.
- aea612a: `Switch` takes `size="sm"`, a 20 px track (`h-5 w-9`) with a 16 px thumb for dense settings rows (#758); the default size renders exactly as before.
- 23ee429: The sale shows how its tax was worked out. `@tallyui/pos` adds `taxDerivation(settings, location)`, a pure function that explains the sale's tax rates: the "tax based on" setting, the address it used (and any fallback, such as shipping to billing), and each tax class with its effective rate (compound rates included) and the rates that matched, with the reason for each match (postcode, city, state, country or anywhere). When nothing matches, it says why: the order has no address, or the store has no rate for this country or address. `SaleTaxLocation` types the sale's `taxLocation`.

  `@tallyui/components` adds `TaxBasedOn`, the "Tax based on" trigger and popover that shows that derivation in three steps (setting, address, rates), with optional "Change in Settings › Tax" and "View all tax rates" links. It also adds `TaxCalculationSettings`, the Settings › Tax "Calculation" group with a "Calculate tax based on" select and read-only rows for prices entered with tax, the shipping tax class and rounding at subtotal, which are set in WooCommerce.

- 4fef712: `Catalogue` takes `variationsStyle`, v2's Product settings › Variations (#719). The default `'list'` keeps the variant list chooser; `'popover'` opens a grid tile's popover for a product with option groups, where the cashier picks one value per group (values no variant allows are dimmed), sees the matched variant's stock, and presses "Add to Cart: \{price\}". A full match never adds on its own, and the popover closes after Add. A product without option groups, and the table view, keep the list chooser. Adds `VariationPicker` to `@tallyui/components`, and `variantFits`, `matchVariant`, `disabledOptions` and `OptionPicks` to `@tallyui/pos`.

#### Patch Changes

- 9dda986: `CartNoteInput` now calls the sale note "Customer note", since WooCommerce sends it as `customer_note` and it prints on the customer's emails and receipts. The default link reads "Add customer note" (was "Add note") and the default placeholder is "Customer note" (was "Order note..."). A new `label` prop names the note for the link, the placeholder and the input's accessibility label. Apps passing their own `placeholder` keep it. Orders now labels the note "Customer note: …" (was "Note: …"), and the line under a note the store could not take reads "The customer note couldn't be sent to the store." on the receipt and in Orders.
- ceec37c: `DialogTitle` keeps its `leading-none` when a caller passes a text size such as `text-base`, and hands the merged class to `Text` children; a caller's own `leading-*` still wins.
- 9f8d06f: Lighter control borders and a card fill, matching WCPOS v2 (#756). In the light theme `--color-input` is now `#d1d5db` (was `#848a94`), so inputs, selects, toggles and outline buttons get a light grey border that still stands out more than `--color-border`; the dark theme's value is unchanged. The outline variant of `Button` and `IconButton`, and `SelectTrigger`, now fill with `bg-card` instead of `bg-background`, so they read as white controls on a white card in the light theme and as card-coloured controls in the dark theme. `Input`, `Textarea` and the `Combobox` trigger fill with `bg-card` too, so every bordered control matches.

### @tallyui/pos

#### Minor Changes

- d6c93e9: A camera barcode scanner (#720, v2's scan button and inline camera panel). `Catalogue` takes `cameraScan` (default false) and `cameraScanner`; with both it puts a `CameraScanButton` beside the search box that opens a `CameraScannerPanel`. The panel calls no pos hook: the app owns `useCameraScan` (inside its `ScanHubProvider`) and passes `{ onDetected: camera.offer, onOpen: camera.reset, scanCount: camera.scanCount }`, so accepted decodes go to the scan hub as `{ source: { kind: 'camera' } }` and the app's `useBarcodeScanner` receives them like device scans. The panel shows v2's status copy, a permission card for native apps, a flash on each `scanCount` change, and a resize handle with increment and decrement actions and a web pointer drag, reporting the height through `onHeightChange`. On the web, `ScannerViewfinder` streams `getUserMedia` into a `<video>` and decodes with the `BarcodeDetector` global when present, or with an injected `decoder` of the same `detect` shape; native apps pass their own `viewfinder`. `@tallyui/pos` adds `useCameraScan` (returning `CameraScan`: `offer`, `reset`, `scanCount`), the pure `startDecodeLoop`, `nativeBarcodeDetector`, `cameraErrorStatus`, `detectorFormatToSymbology` and their constants, and `createScanSession` takes `continuous`, under which a refused repeat restarts its cooldown, so an item resting in view is scanned once.
- 44e9e5f: `Cart` takes `columns`, v2's Cart Settings › Columns (#695): each of Qty, Image, Item, SKU, Price and Total has a `show` switch, `quantity.split` adds a Split link under a quantity above 1, and `name.sku` puts the SKU under the name. The defaults keep today's cart (Qty, Item, Price folding as before, Total). `CartColumnHeads` and `CartLine` take the same `columns`, and `CartLine` takes `sku`, `imageUrl` and `onSplit`; `cartColumnIds` gives the visible columns. `OrderBuilder.splitLine` and `useSale().splitLine` turn a line of quantity N into N lines of 1, refusing a line with a fixed discount (`SPLIT_FIXED_DISCOUNT`). `cartColumnIds` and `CartColumnSettings` are exported from the `@tallyui/components` root.
- fab0922: The second-device customer display's client (ADR-085 U5a, #684, #705), exported from the package root. `createDisplayClient` claims a pairing code, shows the check code while the till's cashier confirms, then collects and keeps the display's token through an injected `DisplayTokenStore`. Once paired it drains its signalling inbox, answers each offer from its till with a `createDisplayPeerLink`, says `display.hello` until the matching `display.config` arrives, and shows what the till sends. While no link is open it reads the snapshot fallback when the advertisement turns it on, applying a body only when its `seq` is not lower than the last one applied. A refused token clears the stored token and the screen and goes back to `enter-code` with `forgotten`; a `network` or `server` failure only marks the display offline. `useDisplayClient` gives its `phase` and `state` to React.
- b05db01: The second-device customer display's WebRTC transport (ADR-085 U3, #684), exported from the package root. `createTillPeerLink` (the offerer) and `createDisplayPeerLink` (the answerer) give a `PeerLink`: a `DisplayTransport` over one ordered data channel labelled `DISPLAY_CHANNEL`, carrying the display envelopes unchanged, with a `state` of `connecting`, `open` or `closed` and `onStateChange`. Signalling goes through an injected `SignalPort`, and the connection comes from an injected `PeerFactory` (the browser's `RTCPeerConnection` by default). ICE candidates that arrive before the remote description are queued and applied after it, in order; a malformed or unexpected signal is ignored; a failed step, a failed connection or a closed channel closes the link for good.
- 6a4529b: `createHidScanner` reads barcodes from a scanner in USB HID Point-of-Sale mode (usage page `0x8C`) through WebHID as a framework-agnostic controller, saving a scanner profile on connect and silently reopening the first granted device that matches a saved profile (#682).
- 434c143: order.create version 8 taxes a sale on the customer's address (#722, step 2). Version 8 is version 7 plus `payload.taxAddress` (`'billing'` or `'shipping'`, the address the till matched the rates against) and the customer's `billing` and `shipping` addresses. A till sends 8 only when a sale was taxed on one of the customer's addresses, and never downgrades such a sale: capped below 8, `toOrderCreateEnvelope` throws `UnsupportedOrderVersionError(8, cap)` and the outbox sends it at 8, never at a version whose server would tax it on the base. A sale taxed on the base sends the same bytes as before. The server precheck refuses `taxAddress` or customer addresses below 8, and 8 without `taxAddress`.

  `@tallyui/pos`: `saleTaxContexts(settings, { orderCreateVersion })` re-matches rates by the customer's address only from order.create 8 (`TAX_ADDRESS_ORDER_CREATE_VERSION`); without the option, or below 8, every customer gets the store's context. `finalizeOrder` takes `taxLocation` and stores the tax address with the customer's addresses. `useSale` refuses a customer whose rates differ from the sale's once tender has begun (`CUSTOMER_RETAX_REFUSED`), telling the cashier to cancel the payment and change the customer in the cart, or start a new order. This is an interim: #759 re-taxes the sale on a customer change, as WCPOS v2 does. `pos_orders` moves to schema 12; its migration is the identity, and a schema-11 till opening a schema-12 store finds no orders.

  `@tallyui/connector-woocommerce`: `wooStoreSettings` fills `taxRateTable` whenever the store has a base address and advertises order.create 8 where it advertised 7. `toWooOrderPayload` sends a version-8 sale's tax address as WCPOS's `_woocommerce_pos_tax_based_on` meta, with the customer's billing and shipping addresses.

- 61c02b9: Add `OrderFilterBar`, WCPOS v2's row of order filter pills (Status, Payment Method, Customer, Cashier, Store / Created via, Register, Date range) with Clear all. It is presentational: the app passes the value, the connector's `orderFilters` and every option list, and a pill shows only when its field is supported. pos adds `OrderFilterValue` and `orderFiltersQuery(value, timezone)`, which turns the bar's value into `ListOrdersQuery` filter fields, with the store-day range as UTC instants (`before` exclusive).
- 94040f9: `methodForProvider` and `methodDrivers` pair the store's payment methods with registered payment drivers, as WCPOS v2's app does.
- e2be8d1: `RegisterPanel` takes `presentation="side"`, WCPOS v2's right-hand register sheet (#716): a header with the store, "open since", the session's age and the opener; the cash expected in large type "in the drawer"; Session sales with the sales count; the Paid in, Paid out and No sale tiles; the drawer ledger (Float, Cash sales, Cash refunds, Paid in, Paid out and No sale, each with its count, then Expected in the drawer); Other tenders; and a footer with Print X-report and Close register. Blind counting hides every figure. The new `storeName`, `onPrintXReport` and `now` props serve the side sheet; the default `presentation="dialog"` is unchanged. `DialogContent` takes `side="right"`. `deriveDrawerTerms` gives `deriveExpected`'s figures term by term with row counts and session sales, and `useRegisterSession` returns them as `terms`.
- edeadc3: `createSaleDisplayPublisher` and `useSaleDisplay` send the live sale to a customer-display `DisplayLink`, with `derivePaymentEvent` ported from WCPOS v2.
- 8352f75: The sale taxes by the customer's address (#722). `saleTaxContexts(settings)` returns `useSale`'s new `taxContextFor` option: the tax context for a customer's address as WooCommerce's "Calculate tax based on" picks it, from `taxRateTable`, cached so that customers matching the same rates share one context (the store's own context for everyone without `taxRateTable` and `baseTaxAddress`). With it, each new sale and each resumed sale is taxed for its customer, and `setCustomer` re-taxes a cart whose new customer moves the rates, keeping its order id, lines, discounts, fees, shipping, note and coupons; a sale in tender, on its receipt or holding captured or moving money keeps its rates, as in WCPOS v2, and a customer set during tender is taxed for when `cancelTender()` brings the cart back. `sale.taxLocation` reports the address used, with `hasCustomer`. `CustomerSummary` gains optional `billing` and `shipping` addresses, kept on the open sale and its draft and never sent. `createTaxContext(props)` builds `TaxProvider`'s context outside React.
- 5afa4d2: `ScanHubProvider` keeps the Web Serial and WebHID scanner connections alive across screens and exposes them through `useScanHub` and `useDeviceScanControls`, and `useBarcodeScanner` now merges those device scans with keyboard-wedge scans, dropping cross-source duplicates with `scanTimeOf` and reporting each through the new `onScanEvent` option (#682).
- d3495c4: The scanner adds a scan hub that merges input sources (`createScanHub`, `createScanBus`, `inertScanHub`), a `dedupeScans` operator that drops one physical scan reaching the POS through two source kinds within `SCAN_DEDUP_WINDOW_MS`, and a second `onScan` argument on the wedge detector, `WedgeScanMeta` (`terminated`, `startedAtMs`).
- 5cd2720: Add scanner device identity keys, a serial line decoder, a HID POS report decoder, Web Serial and WebHID feature detection, and a device-chooser cancel check, ported from WCPOS v2.
- 1ce3343: A device-local `scanner_profiles` collection keyed on `scannerDeviceKey()`, with helpers to read, observe, save (upsert) and remove scanner profiles (#682).
- fa69171: `createSerialScanner` reads barcodes from a USB-serial or Bluetooth SPP scanner through Web Serial as a framework-agnostic controller, saving a scanner profile on connect and silently reopening the one granted port that matches a saved profile (#682).
- 2e691f2: `OrderBuilder.setCoupons` takes a store-priced coupon context (`StorePricedCouponContext`): the store's per-line amounts come off the lines in any rounding mode, `Order.coupons` and `display.coupons` carry each code's share of the settlement totals, and `couponPricesStale` flags an order changed since the store priced it, which `finalizeOrder` refuses; `couponPricingBasis` gives what the prices depend on. `parseInfoCapabilities` reads `coupons: true` from `/tally/v1/info` (#709).
- 73caa25: `createStorePricedDiscountCodes({ supports?, price })` gives `useSale` discount codes for a store that prices its own codes, Medusa promotions first. When the order's store-priced coupon prices go stale, `useSale` prices its accepted codes again (`couponsRepricing`, `repriceCoupons()` for Retry), and `startTender` and `complete()` refuse with `COUPONS_REPRICING` until it has. `find` may now return a `transient` refusal, such as `COUPONS_NEED_CONNECTION`.
- 23ee429: The sale shows how its tax was worked out. `@tallyui/pos` adds `taxDerivation(settings, location)`, a pure function that explains the sale's tax rates: the "tax based on" setting, the address it used (and any fallback, such as shipping to billing), and each tax class with its effective rate (compound rates included) and the rates that matched, with the reason for each match (postcode, city, state, country or anywhere). When nothing matches, it says why: the order has no address, or the store has no rate for this country or address. `SaleTaxLocation` types the sale's `taxLocation`.

  `@tallyui/components` adds `TaxBasedOn`, the "Tax based on" trigger and popover that shows that derivation in three steps (setting, address, rates), with optional "Change in Settings › Tax" and "View all tax rates" links. It also adds `TaxCalculationSettings`, the Settings › Tax "Calculation" group with a "Calculate tax based on" select and read-only rows for prices entered with tax, the shipping tax class and rounding at subtotal, which are set in WooCommerce.

- 41aa864: WooCommerce's tax location, the pure pieces (#722). `woocommerceTax.orderTaxLocation(base, taxBasedOn, order?)` resolves the address an order is taxed by, as `WC_Abstract_Order::get_tax_location()` does: `shipping` falls back to billing and then to the shop base, `billing` to the shop base, and the order's `_woocommerce_pos_tax_based_on` (`TAX_BASED_ON_META_KEY`) overrides the store's basis when valid; `normalizeTaxBasedOn` turns anything else into `base`. `StoreSettings` gains three optional fields a connector fills: `taxBasedOn`, `baseTaxAddress` and `taxRateTable` (the unfiltered WooCommerce rate table). `taxSettingsForLocation(settings, location)` in `@tallyui/pos` re-derives `taxRates` and `taxRatesPpm` for an address from `taxRateTable` (a class's ppm includes compound rates; a class with no matching rate is 0), and returns the settings unchanged when there is no table. Nothing uses them yet.
- b3924e8: The till's second-device display service (ADR-085 U4, #684), exported from the package root. `createTillDisplayService` takes a connector's `DisplaySignalling`, the cashier session, the till's device id, the store's `DisplayAdvertisement` and a `configFor` per display. It lists the displays this till owns every `LIST_POLL_MS` and on `refresh()`, keeps one WebRTC link and one display link per display, and fans each `showCart` and `showPayment` out to all of them (d9), replaying the current sale into a link made later. A link that drops, or stays connecting past `CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS`, is replaced at the next successful list. While a display's channel is not open, `createSnapshotSender` posts its snapshot fallback (d8): at most one POST a second on the trailing edge, the same body again every 20 s, no re-send of a refused body, the 200 KiB cap with the note cut first, no POST for a display the registry reports offline, and an idle snapshot that clears a recent cart from the server. When the server is lost the service goes `offline` (d11): open links carry on, and no link is made and no snapshot posted until a list succeeds. `forbidden`, or no cashier session, pauses every server call; `not_found` drops that display. The service is a `DisplayLink`, so `createSaleDisplayPublisher` drives it.
- b0901ea: The till's pairing flow for a second-device display (ADR-085 U5, d4, d5, #684), exported from the package root. `createTillPairing` takes a connector's `DisplaySignalling`, the cashier session, the till's device id and, optionally, the till display service. `start()` mints a code and shows it with its expiry; while it shows, the claims on it are listed every `PENDING_POLL_MS`, each with its display's check code (#705), and at its expiry the pairing goes idle with `expired`. `confirm` pairs a claim and refreshes the display service; `decline` drops it; `forget` removes a paired display and refreshes the service, and a refused forget shows in `forgetError`. Every refusal is named (`forbidden`, `limit`, `expired`, `not_found`, `network`, `server`), and with no cashier session nothing is called. `useTillPairing` and `useTillDisplays` render a pairing and a display service in React.
- 4fef712: `Catalogue` takes `variationsStyle`, v2's Product settings › Variations (#719). The default `'list'` keeps the variant list chooser; `'popover'` opens a grid tile's popover for a product with option groups, where the cashier picks one value per group (values no variant allows are dimmed), sees the matched variant's stock, and presses "Add to Cart: \{price\}". A full match never adds on its own, and the popover closes after Add. A product without option groups, and the table view, keep the list chooser. Adds `VariationPicker` to `@tallyui/components`, and `variantFits`, `matchVariant`, `disabledOptions` and `OptionPicks` to `@tallyui/pos`.
- 375a8ce: Print a store's own receipt template from a sale. `wcposSourceFromSentOrder`, `wcposSourceFromPosOrder` and `wcposSourceFromStoreOrder` turn the order as sent, the till's stored order and an order read from the store into a WCPOS receipt source, `wcposReceiptContext(config, { pricesIncludeTax })` fills WCPOS's store settings and app context from `ReceiptConfig`, and `renderStoreReceiptTemplate(template, sale, context)` renders a fetched `wcpos` or `tallyui` receipt template, html or thermal.
- 152277c: Add WCPOS v2's receipt-data vocabulary. `wcposReceiptData(source, store, options)` turns a sale in minor units into WCPOS's canonical receipt data (money in major units, the `_incl`/`_excl` twins, savings, the tax summary, fiscal and register blocks), and `formatWcposReceiptData(data)` adds the `*_display` strings, tax-ID labels and the merged `i18n` that WCPOS templates print. `formatWcposMoney`, `WCPOS_DEFAULT_I18N` and `WCPOS_TAX_ID_LABELS` are exported too.

### @tallyui/theme

#### Patch Changes

- 9f8d06f: Lighter control borders and a card fill, matching WCPOS v2 (#756). In the light theme `--color-input` is now `#d1d5db` (was `#848a94`), so inputs, selects, toggles and outline buttons get a light grey border that still stands out more than `--color-border`; the dark theme's value is unchanged. The outline variant of `Button` and `IconButton`, and `SelectTrigger`, now fill with `bg-card` instead of `bg-background`, so they read as white controls on a white card in the light theme and as card-coloured controls in the dark theme. `Input`, `Textarea` and the `Combobox` trigger fill with `bg-card` too, so every bordered control matches.

### @tallyui/connector-woocommerce

#### Minor Changes

- a3387fc: Customers carry an optional `createdAt`, and the optional connector method `searchCustomersPage` returns a page of a customer search with the store's total (`CustomerPage`); the WooCommerce connector fills both, from `date_created_gmt` and the `X-WP-Total` header (#680).
- 434c143: order.create version 8 taxes a sale on the customer's address (#722, step 2). Version 8 is version 7 plus `payload.taxAddress` (`'billing'` or `'shipping'`, the address the till matched the rates against) and the customer's `billing` and `shipping` addresses. A till sends 8 only when a sale was taxed on one of the customer's addresses, and never downgrades such a sale: capped below 8, `toOrderCreateEnvelope` throws `UnsupportedOrderVersionError(8, cap)` and the outbox sends it at 8, never at a version whose server would tax it on the base. A sale taxed on the base sends the same bytes as before. The server precheck refuses `taxAddress` or customer addresses below 8, and 8 without `taxAddress`.

  `@tallyui/pos`: `saleTaxContexts(settings, { orderCreateVersion })` re-matches rates by the customer's address only from order.create 8 (`TAX_ADDRESS_ORDER_CREATE_VERSION`); without the option, or below 8, every customer gets the store's context. `finalizeOrder` takes `taxLocation` and stores the tax address with the customer's addresses. `useSale` refuses a customer whose rates differ from the sale's once tender has begun (`CUSTOMER_RETAX_REFUSED`), telling the cashier to cancel the payment and change the customer in the cart, or start a new order. This is an interim: #759 re-taxes the sale on a customer change, as WCPOS v2 does. `pos_orders` moves to schema 12; its migration is the identity, and a schema-11 till opening a schema-12 store finds no orders.

  `@tallyui/connector-woocommerce`: `wooStoreSettings` fills `taxRateTable` whenever the store has a base address and advertises order.create 8 where it advertised 7. `toWooOrderPayload` sends a version-8 sale's tax address as WCPOS's `_woocommerce_pos_tax_based_on` meta, with the customer's billing and shipping addresses.

- f819768: `receiptTemplates()` lists the store's renderable print templates; WooCommerce reads them from the WCPOS templates route and labels them with the `wcpos` data model.
- 3a6eb07: `SyncContext` takes an optional `fetch`, resolved per request and defaulting to `globalThis.fetch`, so a host can pass a wrapper such as `meteredFetch` to count requests.
  The WooCommerce connector sends every pull, reconcile, settings, payment-methods, receipt-templates and customers request through `context.fetch` when it is set.
- 564433e: `listOrders` now filters order history on the server. The connector declares `orderFilters` (`status`, `customerId`, `cashierId`, `storeId`, `createdVia`, `dateRange`, also exported as `WOO_ORDER_FILTERS`) and sends them to WCPOS `wcpos/v2/orders` as `status`, `customer`, `pos_cashier`, `pos_store`, `created_via` and `after`/`before`, with `dates_are_gmt=true` and the bounds converted to WooCommerce's strict whole-second comparison. `registerId` and `paymentMethod`, which WCPOS does not filter on, are refused with `UnsupportedOrderFilterError` before any request instead of being dropped, and an id that is not all digits, or an empty `status` or `createdVia`, resolves an empty page without a request.
- 5ebdb30: The WooCommerce connector's `paymentMethods` lists the store's POS-enabled payment gateways from WCPOS's `wcpos/v2/payment-methods` route, with money limits and cash rounding in minor units, and falls back to cash and the card terminal on a plugin older than 2.0.0 (#679).
- be21652: A sale's note reaches the WooCommerce order as its customer note (order.create 7, ADR-083), on stores that take coupon orders; on other stores the till shows the note as not sent.
- 13708fa: The WooCommerce connector reads the store's tax basis and base tax address (#722, step 1). When the store calculates taxes, `wooStoreSettings` sets `taxBasedOn` from the store's `tax_based_on` in `wcpos/v2/stores` (normalised, so anything other than `base`, `billing` or `shipping` reads as `base`), and `baseTaxAddress` to the same address it already matches rates against (`tax_address` when it has a country, else the `store_*` fields), with each field a string. It leaves `baseTaxAddress` out when neither address has a country, and sets neither field when taxes are off. These fields are for display only. The connector does not fill `taxRateTable` and does not tax by the customer's address, so the rates the cart uses and every amount stay the same. Per-customer taxing comes later, together with an order.create change so that the server agrees.

#### Patch Changes

- 369b380: Variable products from WCPOS 1.10.20 and later no longer fail validation in dev builds: a product's stored `meta_data` now keeps only the entries with string values, so object meta such as `_woocommerce_pos_variable_prices` is dropped (#713).

## 3.18.0

### @tallyui/core

#### Minor Changes

- 455c39f: `StoreSettings` gains an optional `allTaxRates`: every tax rate the store defines, with where it applies, for an app's "View all tax rates" table (`StoreTaxRate`, `StoreTaxRateLocation`). The WooCommerce connector fills it from the `/taxes` rates it already reads; the filtered `taxRates` and `taxRatesPpm` the cart uses are unchanged (#669).

### @tallyui/pos

#### Minor Changes

- 6067d23: `CurrencyProvider` takes an optional `format` (symbol position, thousand separator, decimal separator, decimals), so `useCurrencyFormatter` can show money the way a store has set it, as WCPOS v2 does. Without `format`, money reads exactly as before (#667).
- 4e0fbb3: `useSale` takes an optional `defaultCustomer`, put on every new sale it starts (the first, and each one after complete, discard, park or `newSale()`), never on a resumed sale, as WCPOS v2 pre-selects its default customer on every new cart. The app decides which customer; without the option nothing changes (#668).

### @tallyui/connector-woocommerce

#### Minor Changes

- 455c39f: `StoreSettings` gains an optional `allTaxRates`: every tax rate the store defines, with where it applies, for an app's "View all tax rates" table (`StoreTaxRate`, `StoreTaxRateLocation`). The WooCommerce connector fills it from the `/taxes` rates it already reads; the filtered `taxRates` and `taxRatesPpm` the cart uses are unchanged (#669).

### @tallyui/pos

#### Minor Changes

- 4a867a9: Log entries can now say who did something (`actor`) and how one record's sync went (`syncRecord`). Register events carry the cashier, and the order outbox logs each order the store accepts, refuses or keeps failing. New `presetFilters`, `hasActor` and `deriveStuckRecords` helpers let a Logs screen show a person's actions and the orders that are still stuck (#656).
- 1297f1c: `useSale().add()` now says whether the entry went into the cart: it returns `{ lineId }` for the line it went into, or the refusal message that `error` shows (the sale is being saved, no price in the sale's currency, or an id or tax code the order can't send). Callers that ignore the result don't change (#662).

#### Patch Changes

- 4677f1f: A closure the store refuses because its number is not the next one is now renumbered once from the store's counters and sent again, so the register is no longer stuck. Closures still waiting to send behind it are renumbered with it, and the next closure follows them. If the store refuses the renumbered closure too, it stays refused and shows as a till update that needs attention.

## 3.16.0

### @tallyui/database

#### Patch Changes

- 1ecd1f1: A sign-in error on the pull now holds the replication instead of pausing it: the pull asks the store nothing and a push waits unsent until `resume()`, so rows queued before the first push are no longer lost (#645). `isPaused()` stays `false` during the hold; watch `notice$` for it.
  - @tallyui/core@3.16.0

### @tallyui/components

#### Minor Changes

- 8d0baa4: Components can now be translated: `I18nProvider` takes the store's locale (or uses the device's) and the app's catalogues for other languages, falling back from `fr_CA` to `fr` to the bundled English, and `useT` reads a string by key. The tender pane is the first area to read its strings this way; without a provider it still shows English.

### @tallyui/pos

#### Patch Changes

- 325a540: A till with fresh storage now numbers its next closure after the store's, instead of starting again at 1. The register's closure number and perpetual totals rise to the store's counters, each one on its own, and never go down.
- 89973e5: Adding, voiding or removing a tender now clears a stale sale error, such as an underpaid refusal, once the payment is corrected. A refused call leaves the error as it was, and a dropped terminal reference still shows its own message.
