## 3.15.0

### @tallyui/core

#### Minor Changes

- c2319ab: Fixes the connectivity label and the update-required latch from 3.14.0.

  - **`noResponse`** (`@tallyui/core`, new): a connector tags its fetch rejections with `fetch(url, init).catch(noResponse)`, so that the neutral rule reads them as offline. A bare `TypeError` no longer counts as offline, since our own code throws those too; a platform network code such as `ECONNREFUSED`, on the error or its `cause`, still does.
  - **Any answer from the store ends offline:** an error with a numeric `status`, such as a 401 or a 404, shows the store is reachable.
  - **The latch holds a push without dropping it.** While it is set, the pull returns empty pages and a push waits, with one error per latch and no retries, and neither calls the store. A push already in flight finishes and is not sent again. `resetUpdateRequired()` re-probes: the pull asks the store again and held writes go in order.
  - **A pause no longer loses or doubles a push** that is held, in flight or waiting to retry. The push waits, before and after the send, until the replication starts again. `startReplication` now retries a failed push itself, still with one error per failed attempt, so a pause during that wait loses no rows. This includes the pause after a sign-in error on the pull. Only a cancel ends the wait.

  Known limit: if a paused replication is cancelled after its rows were sent, the next replication sends them again, because RxDB never recorded them.

### @tallyui/database

#### Patch Changes

- c2319ab: Fixes the connectivity label and the update-required latch from 3.14.0.

  - **`noResponse`** (`@tallyui/core`, new): a connector tags its fetch rejections with `fetch(url, init).catch(noResponse)`, so that the neutral rule reads them as offline. A bare `TypeError` no longer counts as offline, since our own code throws those too; a platform network code such as `ECONNREFUSED`, on the error or its `cause`, still does.
  - **Any answer from the store ends offline:** an error with a numeric `status`, such as a 401 or a 404, shows the store is reachable.
  - **The latch holds a push without dropping it.** While it is set, the pull returns empty pages and a push waits, with one error per latch and no retries, and neither calls the store. A push already in flight finishes and is not sent again. `resetUpdateRequired()` re-probes: the pull asks the store again and held writes go in order.
  - **A pause no longer loses or doubles a push** that is held, in flight or waiting to retry. The push waits, before and after the send, until the replication starts again. `startReplication` now retries a failed push itself, still with one error per failed attempt, so a pause during that wait loses no rows. This includes the pause after a sign-in error on the pull. Only a cancel ends the wait.

  Known limit: if a paused replication is cancelled after its rows were sent, the next replication sends them again, because RxDB never recorded them.

### @tallyui/components

#### Minor Changes

- 233cf61: Adds `CartFoot`, WCPOS v2's cart foot: a "Checkout \{total\}" button beside a ⋮ menu of actions the app passes as `{ label, onPress, destructive? }` items. `Cart` gains an optional `checkout` prop that shows this foot in place of its Cash and Card terminal buttons; without it, Cart is unchanged.
- 77f3612: Cart can now open WCPOS v2's keypad from a line's quantity or price with `lineEditor="popover"`, and the price keypad carries the quick-discount keys. The keypad is a new shared `Keypad` component, also used by the tender pane, and `LinePopover` is exported for apps that build their own lines.

## 3.14.0

### @tallyui/core

#### Minor Changes

- c2319ab: Adds a connectivity label and an update-required latch.

  - **`classifyFailure()`:** a connector may map its own request failures to `offline`, `degraded` or an update-required refusal with this optional method. A connector without it gets a neutral rule: no response is offline, and a timeout or a 5xx is degraded.
  - **`createConnectivityTracker`** (`@tallyui/database`) turns request outcomes into a label for the UI. After 30 seconds of failures it reads `offline` or `degraded`, and it goes back to `online` on the first success. The label only reports: being offline or degraded does not stop replication.
  - **The update-required latch** is the only thing that holds sync. While it is set, `startReplication` neither pulls nor pushes, so unsent writes wait in the queue. It stays set until `resetUpdateRequired()` is called.

### @tallyui/database

#### Minor Changes

- c2319ab: Adds a connectivity label and an update-required latch.

  - **`classifyFailure()`:** a connector may map its own request failures to `offline`, `degraded` or an update-required refusal with this optional method. A connector without it gets a neutral rule: no response is offline, and a timeout or a 5xx is degraded.
  - **`createConnectivityTracker`** (`@tallyui/database`) turns request outcomes into a label for the UI. After 30 seconds of failures it reads `offline` or `degraded`, and it goes back to `online` on the first success. The label only reports: being offline or degraded does not stop replication.
  - **The update-required latch** is the only thing that holds sync. While it is set, `startReplication` neither pulls nor pushes, so unsent writes wait in the queue. It stays set until `resetUpdateRequired()` is called.

### @tallyui/components

#### Minor Changes

- d28e6ea: Adds the app shell from WCPOS v2: `AppShell` puts a `NavRail` beside the page on windows at least 768 wide and 480 tall, and a `NavDrawer` on phones, with `aria-current` on the active item and a focus-trapped drawer that closes on Escape or a backdrop press. `RegisterBar` gains optional `storeName`, `cashier`, `syncStatus` and `onOpenMenu` props, and shows the menu button on its own inside a phone shell.
- 01580b8: `Cart` gains an optional `orderActions` prop, `true` by default. Pass `false` when the app hosts the Order discount and Add charge controls in its own menu, as WCPOS v2's cart "+" menu does; applied order discounts, fees and shipping still show with their Remove controls.
- d544736: Add a customer display that runs in a second window on the same device: a BroadcastChannel transport, a till-side display link that answers the display's hello and returns it to idle after a payment, a `useCustomerDisplay` hook, and the `CustomerDisplayScreen` component.
- 90463af: Adds what an app needs to confirm a cashier switch, a register switch or a sign-out: `switchWarnings()` lists what the switch leaves behind (unsent sales and till updates, parked sales, a partly paid sale, an open register), and `handoverSlip()` builds the hand-over slip with a printable X-report envelope. In `@tallyui/components`, `SwitchConfirm` asks with WCPOS v2's wording and names each warning, and `HandoverSlip` shows the slip.
- 1a5af04: The cart line's Price form can show WCPOS v2's quick-discount keys (`quickDiscounts` on `PriceForm` and `Cart`): a key takes its percentage off the line's current price and Apply writes it. The pos app settings gain `cart.quickDiscounts`, defaulting to v2's `[5, 10, 15]` and keeping only percentages above 0 and up to 100.
- 30cfc94: `RegisterCount`'s `approve` now receives the count and may return the store's `approval` and the `approvedVariance`, which go to `closeSession`, so a store-verified manager approval works through the shared component; a count edited during approval asks again, and zero-argument `approve` callers keep working. `RegisterPanel` shows an "Approval needed" card for a closure the store refused, and its new `reapprove` prop sends a new approval through `reapproveClosure`.
- f68c1d9: `Tender` is redrawn as WCPOS v2's tender pane, with a balance headline that counts captured payments only, each payment leg with its status and Cancel payment where the method can void it, a keypad, and quick-cash chips for the exact amount and the next 5, 10 and 50; `TenderPane` is the same pane for a host that owns the tender reducer. `SplitTender` is now the same component as `Tender`, and `CashTendered`'s default quick amounts round up to 5, 10 and 50.
- cdac1c6: The cart now has WCPOS v2's column heads (Qty, Item, Price, Total) through the new `CartColumnHeads`, and hides the Price column on a narrow cart or a phone. `CartLine` shows a variation's attributes under its name and, with the new `regularUnitPrice` prop, the regular price struck through when the line costs less.
- d96bfc2: Add WCPOS v2's catalogue tile details: `InCartBadge` shows how many of a product the cart holds (summed by the new `cartQuantities` from `@tallyui/pos`), `CategoryTile` shows a category's photo full-bleed with an initial fallback, and `ProductFilterBar` brings v2's filter chips with In Stock on by default. `Catalogue` takes `cartLines` and `showFilterBar` to use them.

#### Patch Changes

- 0648b18: The register, parked-sales and refund screens now show a thrown error by its message alone, so a cashier reads "Finish or cancel the sale in progress first." rather than "RegisterTenderInProgressError: Finish or cancel…". An error with no message shows "Something went wrong".

### @tallyui/pos

#### Minor Changes

- d544736: Add a customer display that runs in a second window on the same device: a BroadcastChannel transport, a till-side display link that answers the display's hello and returns it to idle after a payment, a `useCustomerDisplay` hook, and the `CustomerDisplayScreen` component.
- 5f535c8: Adds ESC/POS printing for thermal printers: `encodeEscPos` lays a receipt out in the printer's columns and code page, `escpos` is the command builder it uses, and `createEscPosTransport` sends the bytes to a sink the app supplies (USB, serial, network or Bluetooth) through the print queue.
- 90463af: Adds what an app needs to confirm a cashier switch, a register switch or a sign-out: `switchWarnings()` lists what the switch leaves behind (unsent sales and till updates, parked sales, a partly paid sale, an open register), and `handoverSlip()` builds the hand-over slip with a printable X-report envelope. In `@tallyui/components`, `SwitchConfirm` asks with WCPOS v2's wording and names each warning, and `HandoverSlip` shows the slip.
- 1a5af04: The cart line's Price form can show WCPOS v2's quick-discount keys (`quickDiscounts` on `PriceForm` and `Cart`): a key takes its percentage off the line's current price and Apply writes it. The pos app settings gain `cart.quickDiscounts`, defaulting to v2's `[5, 10, 15]` and keeping only percentages above 0 and up to 100.
- d96bfc2: Add WCPOS v2's catalogue tile details: `InCartBadge` shows how many of a product the cart holds (summed by the new `cartQuantities` from `@tallyui/pos`), `CategoryTile` shows a category's photo full-bleed with an initial fallback, and `ProductFilterBar` brings v2's filter chips with In Stock on by default. `Catalogue` takes `cartLines` and `showFilterBar` to use them.

#### Patch Changes

- 5a33bd9: `comparisonRange` now compares period to date, as WCPOS v2 does: this week so far against the same weekdays of the week before, and this month so far against the same days of the month before (1–10 Nov against 1–10 Oct). Pass the selected preset as the new optional third argument so a short running month is still treated as a month.
- dddbf6a: Ship `@types/mustache` as a dependency, so apps that typecheck `@tallyui/pos` from source no longer fail with TS7016.

### @tallyui/connector-woocommerce

#### Minor Changes

- add0b19: `searchCustomers` now lists a first page of customers, by last name as WCPOS v2's customer picker does, when the query is empty or whitespace, so a customer dropdown can show customers as soon as it opens.

## 3.13.0

### @tallyui/core

#### Minor Changes

- 73387ab: Adds cashier permissions: a connector may describe the signed-in cashier with an optional `cashier()` that returns neutral permissions (`viewReports`, `manageClosures`, `approveVariance`, `refund`, `discount`). In `@tallyui/pos`, `can()` and the `useCashier` hook read them and give the blind-count flag and a Reports gate; a connector without `cashier()` still allows everything, as before.
- 0bf0072: Add an optional, online-only `updateCustomer(context, id, patch)` connector method with a `CustomerPatch` type, and let `Customer` and `CustomerInput` carry billing and shipping addresses (`CustomerAddress`) and tax ids (`CustomerTaxId`).
- 135eda4: `CustomerServiceError` now has an optional `status`: the HTTP status the store answered with, or undefined when no response came. The WooCommerce, Medusa and Vendure connectors set it, so a retry can check the status instead of parsing the message.
- 7e12633: Core now describes a payment method in a backend-neutral way (`PaymentMethodDescriptor`), and a connector may list its methods with an optional `paymentMethods()`. A connector without it still offers cash and the card terminal, through `defaultPaymentMethods()` and `paymentMethodOf()`.
- c31eef8: Add the core types for register contract 3 (ADR-082): `ServerCapabilities.registerApproval` (the store's approval threshold, read from `/tally/v1/info`), an optional opaque `approval` on the closure payload, and the `register_approval_required` and `register_approval_invalid` conflict codes with their data types.
- 9f04b1b: Connectors can now offer the store's register directory through an optional `listRegisters`, which returns each register's id, name, default float and expected close time. `@tallyui/pos` adds `suggestFloat`, which suggests a register's opening float: its default float, else the cash counted at the last close.
- 80c7a6b: A cashier can now add a note to a sale with `useSale().setNote()`; it is kept when the sale is parked and resumed, and cleared by a new sale. The note is sent to the store as `order.create` version 7, only to servers that advertise version 7 (ADR-083).

#### Patch Changes

- dfda46e: `SUPPORTED_ORDER_CREATE_VERSIONS` now lists versions 5 and 6, which the till already sends. A new test keeps it in step with the order.create envelope's version type.

### @tallyui/database

#### Patch Changes

- 6014050: A catalogue reconcile pass that lists no page now skips the deletion check, so it no longer raises a false "kept on this till" message every hour (#376). The WooCommerce catalogue check now restarts in full after its listing fails on a fresh pass, instead of resuming and skipping deletions for a day (#376).

### @tallyui/components

#### Minor Changes

- 2b4e31d: Adds `RefundForm` and `RefundModal`. The cashier picks quantities up to what is still refundable, with restock, shipping, a signed adjustment, a destination and a required reason, and the form hands back the `order.refund` payload parts, priced by `priceRefund`, for the caller to send.
- 31a244c: App settings gain a device-local `ui.theme` (`system`, `light` or `dark`, default `system`). The new `DisplaySettings` component lets the user choose the theme, the UI scale and the font set, and saves each choice through `useAppSettings`.
- 9beba13: Adds open sales as cart tabs: `useSale` gains `switchSale` (it parks the current sale and opens a parked one, or a new one) and `closeSale`, and `openSales` lists the open sales for `OpenSaleTabs`, which shows each sale's customer or contents, its total and a Partly paid chip. A partly paid sale cannot be switched away from or closed until it is paid or its paid legs are voided.
- 5cb82ce: `useRegisterSession` now returns `liveAtStore`, `approvalNeeded` and `closureApprovalStatus`, and `actions.reapproveClosure` sends a closure the store refused for its approval again with a new proof; at register contract 3 a close over the threshold on a session live at the store needs the store's proof, not `approvedBy` alone. The closure sheet shows "Approval not yet verified by the store" beside the approver until the store applies the closure with its proof.
- f52d025: Adds the Reports Sales room (`SalesRoom`, with its hero chart, figure cards and filters bar) to components, and the store-time date presets it uses (`presetRange`, `DATE_PRESETS`) to pos.
- e4299f5: A reopened refused sale now keeps only the cashier's own note, so the "Refused by the store" text never goes out with `order.create` 7; a sale parked by 3.12.0 that still carries that first line has it dropped when the command is built. New `noteDelivery(order)` tells whether a stored sale's note was sent, and `Receipt` and `OrdersList` show the note and say when it couldn't be sent to the store.
- ff03391: Add font choices for the `ui.font` setting: the font-family tokens, face list and type sets (system, instrument, grotesk, fraunces, manrope) in `@tallyui/theme`, and a `FontProvider` in `@tallyui/components` that loads the chosen set through an app-supplied loader so `Text` draws its body or numbers face. No font files ship in the packages; each app bundles the OFL files and passes `loadFonts`.
- 6296a06: Add the UI scale: nine size tokens for each of the compact, regular and spacious steps with Auto picking by window size, pointer floors for controls and rows, an opt-in `@tallyui/theme/scale.css`, and a `ScaleProvider` that applies the stored `ui.scale` setting on native and mirrors it onto the web document root so portals scale too. `Text` now caps the OS text multiplier at `MAX_FONT_SCALE` (1.3) unless a caller overrides it.

### @tallyui/pos

#### Minor Changes

- 41fa7bf: Adds cash rounding to the tender: `roundedDue` rounds a balance to a hand-counted method's rounding increment, with halves rounding up, and `tenderRounding` gives the tender pane the rounded amount due and the signed rounding difference. These are figures only: nothing is added to the sale yet.
- 73387ab: Adds cashier permissions: a connector may describe the signed-in cashier with an optional `cashier()` that returns neutral permissions (`viewReports`, `manageClosures`, `approveVariance`, `refund`, `discount`). In `@tallyui/pos`, `can()` and the `useCashier` hook read them and give the blind-count flag and a Reports gate; a connector without `cashier()` still allows everything, as before.
- 31a244c: App settings gain a device-local `ui.theme` (`system`, `light` or `dark`, default `system`). The new `DisplaySettings` component lets the user choose the theme, the UI scale and the font set, and saves each choice through `useAppSettings`.
- 9beba13: Adds open sales as cart tabs: `useSale` gains `switchSale` (it parks the current sale and opens a parked one, or a new one) and `closeSale`, and `openSales` lists the open sales for `OpenSaleTabs`, which shows each sale's customer or contents, its total and a Partly paid chip. A partly paid sale cannot be switched away from or closed until it is paid or its paid legs are voided.
- e2d3a2b: The register outbox now counts the till updates the store rejected that hold up their register (`state.rejected`), and `requeue()` sends a rejected till update again under its own command id, except for the refusals the store records or that have their own exits (#366). The new `summarizeOutboxHealth`, `watchOutboxHealth` and `useOutboxHealth` give one summary of sales and till updates waiting, rejected or stuck, and whether either outbox needs the cashier to sign in again, with one `flush()` for both (#373).
- 13f8a3b: Each payment on a sale now has a status (pending, authorized, captured, failed or voided). Only captured payments count toward the balance. A payment can be voided and stays on the sale, counting nothing, and a sale with a payment still in progress cannot complete.
- c9ca7a2: Add a device-local app settings store: an `app_settings` collection that is never replicated, a typed `AppSettings` map with defaults (`ui.scale`, `ui.font`), `readAppSettings`/`writeAppSetting`, and a `useAppSettings` hook. Unknown or invalid stored values fall back to their defaults.
- 3b32e4c: Add keyboard-wedge barcode scanning: `useBarcodeScanner` tells a scanner's fast burst of keys from a person typing and calls `onScan` with the code, along with retail barcode helpers ported from WCPOS v2 (UPC-A/EAN-13 leading-zero handling, check digits and a repeat-scan cooldown).
- 6995e84: Add a shared CSV writer (`toCsv`, `csvFromCells`, `csvCell`) that quotes every cell and guards formula-leading text, as WCPOS v2's reports export does. The register's closures export now uses it, with unchanged output.
- d4ae385: Add the customer display protocol: a versioned message envelope with `encodeDisplayMessage` and a `decodeDisplayMessage` that refuses anything it cannot trust, the five display actions, a cart snapshot over `ReceiptData` kept under 200 KiB, and a `displayReducer` for the idle, cart and payment screens.
- 9e0a750: POS now describes a card reader or payment terminal through a backend-neutral `PaymentDriver` interface, with a registry that finds a payment method's driver by its `capture.provider`. A deterministic simulated driver, with injectable timers, approves, declines, cancels and times out on cue for tests and demos.
- 53c6dc8: POS now prints through a `PrintTransport` interface, with `createPrintQueue` running each printer's jobs one at a time so a failed or stalled printer never holds up another. A device-local `printer_profiles` collection stores the printers set up on a till, and `createSystemPrintTransport` prints receipt HTML through the browser's print dialog, or through a `printHtml` function the app passes in on native (such as expo-print).
- 5fedc5d: New receipt templates: `renderReceiptHtml` turns a `ReceiptData` into sanitised HTML with a default 80 mm or 58 mm template, and `receiptTemplateData`, `renderThermalPreview` and `sanitizeHtml` let an app render its own thermal-XML Mustache templates, sanitised with DOMPurify in a browser and parsed with xmldom on React Native. QR codes and barcodes render as placeholders for now.
- d0dc455: Add sales figures for the Reports screen: totals, payments by method, top products and categories, taxes by rate, refunds on the day they were made and a per-day series with a comparison range, all by store day. Adapters read the till's own orders and refunds and the store's orders.
- 9b6d07c: `refundableLines` shows what is still refundable on each order line after earlier refunds, as a quantity plus net and tax amounts. `priceRefund` prices a chosen refund by each line's cumulative share (ADR-080 amendment 2), caps each quantity at what remains, and returns the `order.refund` v1 payload lines.
- 5cb82ce: `useRegisterSession` now returns `liveAtStore`, `approvalNeeded` and `closureApprovalStatus`, and `actions.reapproveClosure` sends a closure the store refused for its approval again with a new proof; at register contract 3 a close over the threshold on a session live at the store needs the store's proof, not `approvedBy` alone. The closure sheet shows "Approval not yet verified by the store" beside the approver until the store applies the closure with its proof.
- d1c587e: At register contract 3 (ADR-082), the closure command is sent as version 3 and carries the store's opaque `approval` proof, which `closeSession` keeps on the session and never puts on the Z, a fact or a log. The store's approval threshold now wins over the app's `varianceThreshold`, and the new `closureVariance` helper and hook action give the variance an approval is bound to; a close whose `approvedVariance` no longer matches is refused.
- 9f04b1b: Connectors can now offer the store's register directory through an optional `listRegisters`, which returns each register's id, name, default float and expected close time. `@tallyui/pos` adds `suggestFloat`, which suggests a register's opening float: its default float, else the cash counted at the last close.
- 4a9f14c: The register outbox no longer lets a closure the store refused for its approval (`register_approval_required` or `register_approval_invalid`) block the register's other commands, but it holds the register's next closure until the refused one is approved again (ADR-082 decision 6). Requeue never resends either refusal, and neither counts in `rejected` or keeps a stuck clock on the held closure.
- f52d025: Adds the Reports Sales room (`SalesRoom`, with its hero chart, figure cards and filters bar) to components, and the store-time date presets it uses (`presetRange`, `DATE_PRESETS`) to pos.
- e4299f5: A reopened refused sale now keeps only the cashier's own note, so the "Refused by the store" text never goes out with `order.create` 7; a sale parked by 3.12.0 that still carries that first line has it dropped when the command is built. New `noteDelivery(order)` tells whether a stored sale's note was sent, and `Receipt` and `OrdersList` show the note and say when it couldn't be sent to the store.
- 80c7a6b: A cashier can now add a note to a sale with `useSale().setNote()`; it is kept when the sale is parked and resumed, and cleared by a new sale. The note is sent to the store as `order.create` version 7, only to servers that advertise version 7 (ADR-083).

### @tallyui/theme

#### Minor Changes

- ff03391: Add font choices for the `ui.font` setting: the font-family tokens, face list and type sets (system, instrument, grotesk, fraunces, manrope) in `@tallyui/theme`, and a `FontProvider` in `@tallyui/components` that loads the chosen set through an app-supplied loader so `Text` draws its body or numbers face. No font files ship in the packages; each app bundles the OFL files and passes `loadFonts`.
- 6296a06: Add the UI scale: nine size tokens for each of the compact, regular and spacious steps with Auto picking by window size, pointer floors for controls and rows, an opt-in `@tallyui/theme/scale.css`, and a `ScaleProvider` that applies the stored `ui.scale` setting on native and mirrors it onto the web document root so portals scale too. `Text` now caps the OS text multiplier at `MAX_FONT_SCALE` (1.3) unless a caller overrides it.

#### Patch Changes

- 09d7140: `tokens.css` no longer wraps its `@variant light` and `@variant dark` blocks in `:root`, so the dark theme applies on web with Uniwind 1.4, which turned the wrapped rule into `:root .dark` and never matched. Token values are unchanged, and both themes still apply on web and native with Uniwind 1.3.

### @tallyui/connector-woocommerce

#### Minor Changes

- 135eda4: `CustomerServiceError` now has an optional `status`: the HTTP status the store answered with, or undefined when no response came. The WooCommerce, Medusa and Vendure connectors set it, so a retry can check the status instead of parsing the message.
- e4e59d7: The WooCommerce connector now has `updateCustomer`: it edits a customer's name, email, phone, company, billing and shipping addresses and tax ids through WCPOS's own `push/customers` update, the same route `createCustomer` uses.

#### Patch Changes

- 6014050: A catalogue reconcile pass that lists no page now skips the deletion check, so it no longer raises a false "kept on this till" message every hour (#376). The WooCommerce catalogue check now restarts in full after its listing fails on a fresh pass, instead of resuming and skipping deletions for a day (#376).

### @tallyui/connector-medusa

#### Minor Changes

- 135eda4: `CustomerServiceError` now has an optional `status`: the HTTP status the store answered with, or undefined when no response came. The WooCommerce, Medusa and Vendure connectors set it, so a retry can check the status instead of parsing the message.
- 5707be4: The Medusa admin-user connector can now edit a customer: `updateCustomer` saves the customer's own fields and replaces their default billing and shipping addresses in Medusa's address book. Customers read with their addresses now carry `billing` and `shipping`.

### @tallyui/connector-vendure

#### Minor Changes

- 135eda4: `CustomerServiceError` now has an optional `status`: the HTTP status the store answered with, or undefined when no response came. The WooCommerce, Medusa and Vendure connectors set it, so a retry can check the status instead of parsing the message.
- e77573e: The Vendure connector now provides `listOrders` and `getOrders`, so the shared order history and receipt reprint work on Vendure. It maps Vendure's orders to `StoreOrder` (new `toVendureStoreOrder`, `createVendureOrders` and `listVendureOrderDetails`); pass `tallyFields: false` to `createVendureConnector` on a server without the vendurepos plugin.
- 58c9f24: The Vendure connector can now edit a customer (`updateCustomer`), using Vendure's `updateCustomer` mutation for names, email and phone and its customer address book for the billing and shipping addresses. Company and tax ids are not stored, because Vendure customers have neither.

## 3.12.0

### @tallyui/core

#### Minor Changes

- b1397a3: `OrderCreateLine` takes `anyAttributes`, the names of the attributes the cashier picked for a variant's "Any" option groups. It is a version-6 field, checked by the precheck and the shape check (#495).
- e32dda1: Order history and reprint (G7, ADR-081). `TallyConnector` gains optional `listOrders` and `getOrders`, which read the store's orders online as `StoreOrder`s, with the store's line ids and prior refunds; a connector without them is unchanged. The WooCommerce connector implements both over WCPOS `wcpos/v2/orders`. `@tallyui/pos` adds `buildReceiptDataFromPosOrder` and `buildReceiptDataFromStoreOrder`, which build a receipt marked as a reprint (`fiscal.is_reprint`, `reprint_count`) from a stored order, and `Receipt` takes a prebuilt `receipt` and a `doneLabel`.

### @tallyui/components

#### Minor Changes

- 6a6e925: `Catalogue` takes `pickAnyOptions`. When it is set, choosing a variant with local "Any" option groups first asks for a value for each group, then passes them to `onSelect` as the entry's `picks` (#495).
- 2076f68: `Cart` takes `couponDiscountMinor`, the coupon amount the app itemises under the Subtotal (`afterSubtotal`). When it is set, the totals' Discount row shows the other discounts only and is hidden when they come to 0, so a coupon is no longer counted twice. Without it, the Discount row is unchanged.
- e32dda1: Order history and reprint (G7, ADR-081). `TallyConnector` gains optional `listOrders` and `getOrders`, which read the store's orders online as `StoreOrder`s, with the store's line ids and prior refunds; a connector without them is unchanged. The WooCommerce connector implements both over WCPOS `wcpos/v2/orders`. `@tallyui/pos` adds `buildReceiptDataFromPosOrder` and `buildReceiptDataFromStoreOrder`, which build a receipt marked as a reprint (`fiscal.is_reprint`, `reprint_count`) from a stored order, and `Receipt` takes a prebuilt `receipt` and a `doneLabel`.
- f52dfcd: `OrdersList` takes `onOpen`. When it is set, each order row, from the till or the store's history, gets an Open button that passes that row to `onOpen`, so an app can open the order and reprint its receipt. A till row's press still expands its lines, and without `onOpen` the list is unchanged.

#### Patch Changes

- 5df00c0: An invalid Any pick is reported as a cart error instead of being thrown. The Any picker closes when its product leaves the catalogue (#495).

### @tallyui/pos

#### Minor Changes

- b1397a3: **Storage, one way: `pos_orders` moves from schema version 9 to 10.** It adds a line's `anyAttributes`, and the migration keeps every order byte for byte. `CatalogueEntry` takes `picks` for a variant's local "Any" option groups. `addEntryToCart` validates them, stores them on the line, and keeps lines with different picks apart. A sale with picks goes at order.create version 6, and every other sale's envelope is unchanged (#495).
- e32dda1: Order history and reprint (G7, ADR-081). `TallyConnector` gains optional `listOrders` and `getOrders`, which read the store's orders online as `StoreOrder`s, with the store's line ids and prior refunds; a connector without them is unchanged. The WooCommerce connector implements both over WCPOS `wcpos/v2/orders`. `@tallyui/pos` adds `buildReceiptDataFromPosOrder` and `buildReceiptDataFromStoreOrder`, which build a receipt marked as a reprint (`fiscal.is_reprint`, `reprint_count`) from a stored order, and `Receipt` takes a prebuilt `receipt` and a `doneLabel`.

#### Patch Changes

- 5df00c0: An invalid Any pick is reported as a cart error instead of being thrown. The Any picker closes when its product leaves the catalogue (#495).

### @tallyui/connector-woocommerce

#### Minor Changes

- 6a6e925: A line's picked "Any" options go to WooCommerce as line meta, keyed as WooCommerce keys a local attribute, so the order shows the cashier's choice instead of an empty value. Every line without picks is sent as before (#495).
- e32dda1: Order history and reprint (G7, ADR-081). `TallyConnector` gains optional `listOrders` and `getOrders`, which read the store's orders online as `StoreOrder`s, with the store's line ids and prior refunds; a connector without them is unchanged. The WooCommerce connector implements both over WCPOS `wcpos/v2/orders`. `@tallyui/pos` adds `buildReceiptDataFromPosOrder` and `buildReceiptDataFromStoreOrder`, which build a receipt marked as a reprint (`fiscal.is_reprint`, `reprint_count`) from a stored order, and `Receipt` takes a prebuilt `receipt` and a `doneLabel`.

## 3.10.0

### @tallyui/core

> **Storage, one way: `pos_orders` moves from schema version 8 to 9.** Every stored order is kept byte for byte, but a till that goes back to a build on version 8 shows no orders and sends none of its pending ones until it is upgraded again. Nothing is deleted.

#### Minor Changes

- 38d3032: A coupon sale is sent as `order.create` version 6 (#501, ADR-077 step d4a). No app passes a coupon source yet, so nothing changes for a cashier.
  - Version 6 is version 5 plus the order's `coupons` (`code`, `couponId`, `discountMinor`, `discountTaxMinor`), each line's `regularUnitPriceMinor` and `attributes` when stored, and the receipt's `display.coupons`. `precheckCommand` accepts it, refuses those fields below version 6, and checks that each receipt coupon row names a coupon in the payload, once.
  - In version 6, `subtotalMinor` and `taxMinor` are net of the coupons, and the top-level `discountMinor` still sums only the lines' discounts.
  - `CommandRejectionCode` gains `coupon_invalid` (with `OrderCreateCouponInvalidData`: `storeCode`, `couponCode`) and `total_mismatch` (with `OrderCreateTotalMismatchData`).
  - Only a coupon sale goes as 6. A sale without coupons is sent as before, byte for byte, whatever the store advertises.
  - A coupon sale never goes below 6: `useSale` offers coupons only when the store also accepts version 6, `finalizeOrder` refuses a coupon sale to a store below 6, and the outbox keeps a coupon sale at 6 after an `unsupported_version` answer, so an older store refuses it instead of receiving it without its coupons.
  - The till's highest `order.create` version (`ORDER_CREATE_MAX_VERSION` in `@tallyui/pos`'s command module, not exported from the package) is 6, and a test pins it to `pos_orders`' `sentVersion` and `downgradedFrom` maxima.

#### Patch Changes

- f50fca2: `CatalogueReconcileAdapter.enqueue` entries may carry `refreshOnly`.

### @tallyui/database

> **Storage, one way: `pos_orders` moves from schema version 8 to 9.** Every stored order is kept byte for byte, but a till that goes back to a build on version 8 shows no orders and sends none of its pending ones until it is upgraded again. Nothing is deleted.

### @tallyui/primitives

> **Storage, one way: `pos_orders` moves from schema version 8 to 9.** Every stored order is kept byte for byte, but a till that goes back to a build on version 8 shows no orders and sends none of its pending ones until it is upgraded again. Nothing is deleted.

### @tallyui/components

> **Storage, one way: `pos_orders` moves from schema version 8 to 9.** Every stored order is kept byte for byte, but a till that goes back to a build on version 8 shows no orders and sends none of its pending ones until it is upgraded again. Nothing is deleted.

#### Patch Changes

- 3fd1ed4: Needs attention offers no Retry for a `coupon_invalid` or `total_mismatch` refusal, which the outbox does not resend (#501, ADR-077 Q-d4b-4).
- 811b1f2: `CustomerPicker`'s search field now has the testID `customer-picker-search`. A new optional `testID` prop overrides it. The accessibility label "Search customers" is unchanged.

### @tallyui/storage-sqlite

> **Storage, one way: `pos_orders` moves from schema version 8 to 9.** Every stored order is kept byte for byte, but a till that goes back to a build on version 8 shows no orders and sends none of its pending ones until it is upgraded again. Nothing is deleted.

### @tallyui/pos

> **Storage, one way: `pos_orders` moves from schema version 8 to 9.** Every stored order is kept byte for byte, but a till that goes back to a build on version 8 shows no orders and sends none of its pending ones until it is upgraded again. Nothing is deleted.

#### Minor Changes

- 8c35723: `pos_orders` is now schema version 9 (#501, ADR-077 step d2). Version 9 declares four optional fields, and nothing writes them yet:

  - the order's `coupons` (`{ code, couponId, discountMinor, discountTaxMinor }`, exported as `PosOrderCoupon`);
  - the receipt's `display.coupons`;
  - a line's `attributes` (#495);
  - a line's `regularUnitPriceMinor`.

  It also lets an order's `sentVersion` and `downgradedFrom` be 6, ready for order.create version 6 (ADR-077 d4).

  It also declares an order's optional `reopenedAt`, for a refused sale the till reopens as a parked sale (ADR-077 d4b).

  The migration from version 8 is the identity, so every stored order is kept byte for byte. This is a one-way storage change (ADR-069). A till that goes back to a build on version 8 shows no orders and sends none of its pending ones until it is upgraded again. Nothing is deleted.

- 74a58a4: The order layer carries coupon ids and regular prices (#501, ADR-077 step d3b). Nothing applies a coupon yet.
  - **Breaking** change for code that builds an `OrderCouponContext` or reads `Order.coupons` as shipped in 3.9.0: `couponIds` is now required and each `Order.coupons` row has a required `couponId`. `setCoupons` refuses a code whose id is missing or empty, and takes an optional new context, which replaces the builder's once every code passes its checks. No consumer outside `@tallyui/pos` builds the context. This remains a minor release because TallyUI is pre-beta.
  - A catalogue line keeps its regular unit price as `regularUnitPriceMinor`. The order builder picks the regular price from `resolvePrice`'s result. A price edit or a restored draft keeps it, lines with different regular prices do not merge, and custom lines have none.
  - Parked-sale drafts keep the order's coupons; restoring a draft does not apply them.
  - `finalizeOrder` stores the order's coupons and each line's regular price in `pos_orders` version 9. An order without them is stored as before.
  - Coupons that exclude sale items are still refused.
- c2efd7d: The sale applies coupons (#501, ADR-077 step d3c). No app passes a coupon source yet, so nothing changes for a cashier.
  - `useSale` returns `applyCoupon(code)` and `removeCoupon(code)`. `applyCoupon` refuses with `COUPONS_UNSUPPORTED` ("This store's plugin does not support coupons yet") unless the store's capabilities say `coupons: true`, the app passes discount codes (`discountCodes`, see the d6 entry), and they support the store; WooCommerce's need its tax context.
  - Each code is validated with `woocommerceCoupons.validateCoupon` before it is applied, and every refusal has its own cashier-facing sentence (`woocommerceDiscountCodes.couponRefusal`).
  - Resuming a parked sale looks its coupons up and validates them again; any that no longer pass are removed, and the message says why.
  - `useSale`'s settings may carry `calcDiscountsSequentially`. New export: `COUPONS_UNSUPPORTED`.
- 38d3032: A coupon sale is sent as `order.create` version 6 (#501, ADR-077 step d4a). No app passes a coupon source yet, so nothing changes for a cashier.
  - Version 6 is version 5 plus the order's `coupons` (`code`, `couponId`, `discountMinor`, `discountTaxMinor`), each line's `regularUnitPriceMinor` and `attributes` when stored, and the receipt's `display.coupons`. `precheckCommand` accepts it, refuses those fields below version 6, and checks that each receipt coupon row names a coupon in the payload, once.
  - In version 6, `subtotalMinor` and `taxMinor` are net of the coupons, and the top-level `discountMinor` still sums only the lines' discounts.
  - `CommandRejectionCode` gains `coupon_invalid` (with `OrderCreateCouponInvalidData`: `storeCode`, `couponCode`) and `total_mismatch` (with `OrderCreateTotalMismatchData`).
  - Only a coupon sale goes as 6. A sale without coupons is sent as before, byte for byte, whatever the store advertises.
  - A coupon sale never goes below 6: `useSale` offers coupons only when the store also accepts version 6, `finalizeOrder` refuses a coupon sale to a store below 6, and the outbox keeps a coupon sale at 6 after an `unsupported_version` answer, so an older store refuses it instead of receiving it without its coupons.
  - The till's highest `order.create` version (`ORDER_CREATE_MAX_VERSION` in `@tallyui/pos`'s command module, not exported from the package) is 6, and a test pins it to `pos_orders`' `sentVersion` and `downgradedFrom` maxima.
- 3fd1ed4: A sale the store refused over its coupons can be reopened as a parked sale (#501, ADR-077 step d4b). No app shows the action yet, so nothing changes for a cashier.
  - `reopenRefusedOrder(orderId, deps)` rebuilds a `coupon_invalid` or `total_mismatch` refusal as a parked sale without its coupons. The parked sale carries the order's payments, and its note gives the store's reason. A second call returns the parked sale already written. The refused order is marked with `reopenedAt` and stays as the record. The function refuses with `ReopenRefusedOrderError` when the order is missing, not rejected, refused for another reason, already replaced by a finalized sale, or in a register session that is not open.
  - A refused order counts nowhere once its finalized successor (`saleId`) exists (`replacedOrderIds`): it is left out of Needs attention, the outbox's rejected count, the Z report and the register's live expected figures. Until then it counts like any rejected order. `isReopened` only tells that a reopen was started.
  - `requeue` no longer resends a `coupon_invalid` or `total_mismatch` refusal.
  - `isRequeueable(code)` reports whether the outbox resends a refusal with that code.
- f50fca2: Coupons on one register (ADR-077 d5). `woocommerceDiscountCodes.createSaleCouponSource` gives `useSale` its coupons from the local `coupons` collection. It adds this register's own uses to `usage_count` and `used_by`: a pending order always counts, and an applied order counts until a later pull rewrites the coupon or for at most 10 minutes. `woocommerceDiscountCodes.startCouponUsageRefetch` asks the coupon feed to fetch a coupon again by id once an order using it applies. Neither writes to any collection. The overlay records the customer's WooCommerce id, else the lower-cased email.
- 10a3813: The sale takes the platform's discount codes (ADR-077 amendment 3). Each platform keeps its own discount model, so `useSale`'s `discountCodes` option takes a `SaleDiscountCodes` contract. The contract has five parts: `supports`, `find`, `check`, `apply`, and an optional `beforeResume`. Each part is given a `SaleDiscountState`: the order, a copy of the codes already accepted, the categories and the settings. Both `SaleDiscountCodes` and `SaleDiscountState` are exported as types. A refusal is shown to the cashier in the platform's own words. WooCommerce coupons are one implementation: `woocommerceDiscountCodes.createDiscountCodes(source)`. `createSaleCouponSource`, `startCouponUsageRefetch`, `couponRefusal` and the `SaleCoupon` and `SaleCouponSource` types are exported under `woocommerceDiscountCodes`, not at the top level. A cashier sees no change.

### @tallyui/theme

> **Storage, one way: `pos_orders` moves from schema version 8 to 9.** Every stored order is kept byte for byte, but a till that goes back to a build on version 8 shows no orders and sends none of its pending ones until it is upgraded again. Nothing is deleted.

### @tallyui/connector-woocommerce

> **Storage, one way: `pos_orders` moves from schema version 8 to 9.** Every stored order is kept byte for byte, but a till that goes back to a build on version 8 shows no orders and sends none of its pending ones until it is upgraded again. Nothing is deleted.

#### Minor Changes

- 133faac: The WooCommerce connector sends a coupon sale (#501, ADR-077 step d4a-woo).
  - A store that lists `order_create_v5` and passes the coupons gate (plugin 1.9.0 or later) is advertised `orderCreate: 6`. `WOO_ORDER_CREATE_VERSION` is still 5.
  - `createWooCommandTransport` takes `acceptsCoupons` (a boolean or a function, read at push time). Absent or false, a sale that still carries coupons is refused as `coupon_invalid` (`storeCode: 'coupons_unsupported'`) without a request, so wire it to the store's `coupons` capability to send coupon sales.
  - A version-6 envelope with coupons is pushed with `coupon_lines` (the codes, in the order applied), and each line carries its POS price and regular price in `_woocommerce_pos_data`, so WooCommerce applies the coupons to the till's prices.
  - On a tax-inclusive line, `price` is the gross unit price after the line's own discounts, taken from the till's stored line; without it the order is refused as `unsupported_tax_mode`.
  - A store's coupon refusal (`woocommerce_rest_invalid_coupon`) is rejected as `coupon_invalid`, never retried, with the store's message as plain text, `storeCode`, and `couponCode` when the message names a coupon of the sale.
  - A store total that differs after the order is created stays `applied`, with the store order's refs and the `total_mismatch` warning (ADR-077's R4 exception).
  - An envelope without coupons is pushed exactly as before.

### @tallyui/connector-medusa

> **Storage, one way: `pos_orders` moves from schema version 8 to 9.** Every stored order is kept byte for byte, but a till that goes back to a build on version 8 shows no orders and sends none of its pending ones until it is upgraded again. Nothing is deleted.

> **Storage, one way: `pos_orders` moves from schema version 8 to 9.** Every stored order is kept byte for byte, but a till that goes back to a build on version 8 shows no orders and sends none of its pending ones until it is upgraded again. Nothing is deleted.

### @tallyui/connector-vendure

> **Storage, one way: `pos_orders` moves from schema version 8 to 9.** Every stored order is kept byte for byte, but a till that goes back to a build on version 8 shows no orders and sends none of its pending ones until it is upgraded again. Nothing is deleted.

#### Patch Changes

- 7649149: A refund line's amount is now its cumulative share of the line total, the vendurepos plugin's rule (ADR-080 amendment 2). `getVendureOrder` also reads each line's `proratedLinePriceWithTax`. `vendureRefundable` gives each line `lineTotalWithTax`, and the new `vendureLineRefundWithTax(line, quantity)` returns `round((r + q) × T / N) − round(r × T / N)`. A whole-line refund is now exactly the line total; per-unit `unitRefundWithTax` could miss it by a few minor units, and the plugin refused it with `amount_mismatch`. `unitRefundWithTax` stays for display and is deprecated.

## 3.9.2

### @tallyui/core

#### Patch Changes

- fc37285: `@tallyui/pos` no longer imports from `@tallyui/core/server`, whose barrel reaches `node:crypto`. Browser bundlers such as Expo Snack could not build `@tallyui/pos`, and so `@tallyui/components`, because of it. `parseCommandResult` and `CommandResultError` are now exported from `@tallyui/core` as well as from `@tallyui/core/server`, and pos imports them from there. CI now checks that no browser-facing package's dist reaches `@tallyui/core/server` or a Node built-in.

### @tallyui/pos

#### Patch Changes

- fc37285: `@tallyui/pos` no longer imports from `@tallyui/core/server`, whose barrel reaches `node:crypto`. Browser bundlers such as Expo Snack could not build `@tallyui/pos`, and so `@tallyui/components`, because of it. `parseCommandResult` and `CommandResultError` are now exported from `@tallyui/core` as well as from `@tallyui/core/server`, and pos imports them from there. CI now checks that no browser-facing package's dist reaches `@tallyui/core/server` or a Node built-in.

- d0df307: `OrdersList` shows the store's order history next to the till's own sales (ADR-052 amendment 1). It takes an optional `history` of platform-neutral rows (`OrderHistoryRow`: `id`, `clientOrderId?`, `reference`, `placedAt`, `totalMinor`, `currency`, `itemCount`, `stateLabel`), which the app maps from its own store.

  - A history row whose `clientOrderId` matches an outbox order is hidden while that order is in `orders`.
  - Recent merges the two lists, newest first.
  - A history row is read-only: it has no detail, no Retry, and never appears under Needs attention.

  Without `history`, `OrdersList` renders exactly as before.

## 3.9.0

### @tallyui/core

#### Minor Changes

- 39a4583: `@tallyui/core` exports the WooCommerce coupon engine as `woocommerceCoupons` (#501, ADR-077 phase b), next to `woocommerceTax`. It has `recalculateCoupons`, `validateCoupon`, `toCouponConfigs` and `enrichCategoriesWithAncestors`, with their input, result and rejection types. Nothing in TallyUI calls it yet; the sale builder will in phase (d).
- 3e2245a: `order.refund` version 1 (ADR-080), an online refund command on the command channel. `@tallyui/core` adds the envelope and payload types, `refundPayloadErrors` (the strict shape check), the `order.refund` type in `validateBatch`, an optional `orderRefund` list in `precheckCommand`, the `refund` result in `parseCommandResult`, the refusal codes (`nothing_to_refund`, `quantity_exceeds`, `amount_mismatch`, `order_state`, `not_till_order`, `forbidden`, `no_open_session`), the `orderRefund` capability from `/tally/v1/info`, and refunds in `deriveSessionFigures`: a session's expected figures fall by the refunds it made, and it returns `refundsTotalMinor` when refunds are passed (callers that pass none get the same result as before). `@tallyui/pos` adds `sendOrderRefund(transport, envelope)`, which sends one refund and never queues it. `@tallyui/connector-vendure`'s `getVendureOrder` also reads each line's placed quantity and prorated prices and each refund's amounts and metadata, and `vendureRefundable(order)` computes what is left to refund.
- a3dc028: The order builder can apply WooCommerce coupons (#501, ADR-077 step d1). `createOrderBuilder` takes an optional `couponContext` (the coupon configs, product categories and the store's sequential-discount setting), and `setCoupons(codes)` replays the codes through the ported engine on a WooCommerce tax context. The order then carries `coupons` (`{ code, discountMinor, discountTaxMinor }`), its lines and totals are the post-coupon figures, and `display.coupons` shows one row per coupon while the display lines keep their pre-coupon amounts. Codes are trimmed, lower-cased and deduplicated. An unknown code, or one that excludes sale items, throws a `RangeError`. Other tax contexts ignore the codes. Drafts do not save coupons yet (step d2), and `useSale` does not expose them yet (step d3). `@tallyui/core` also exports `woocommerceCoupons.calculateOrderTotals` and `StoreSettings.calcDiscountsSequentially`, which the WooCommerce connector now reads from the store's settings.
- 006cfb2: `ServerCapabilities` gains an optional `coupons` (#501, ADR-077 ruling R3). Absent means the store does not accept an order's coupons. The WooCommerce connector's `readWooCapabilities` now also reads `GET wcpos/v2/site`. `coupons` is true only when its `wcpos_version` is woocommerce-pos 1.9.0 or later; every unclear or failed read gives false. `orderCreate` is decided as before. Nothing reads `coupons` yet.
- 19093c2: The WooCommerce connector now has a `coupons` collection (#500, ADR-077 amendment 2). Its only source is a catalogue reconcile over the store's published coupons. Each coupon's fingerprint is `date_modified_gmt|usage_count|used_by`, because using a coupon does not move its modified time. A coupon whose fingerprint differs is refetched by id. A draft arrives deleted. A coupon that is trashed or no longer published is removed only after the store confirms it by id. `TallyConnector.reconcile` gains an optional `coupons` key. A connector without it has no coupons. Nothing reads the collection yet, and no app starts the coupons reconcile yet.

#### Patch Changes

- c1bf800: Every exports entry gains a `default` condition so CommonJS and `require`-condition resolvers (Node `require`, webpack, Snack's bundler) resolve the packages; `smoke:pack` checks `require.resolve` for every entry.

### @tallyui/pos

#### Minor Changes

- 3e2245a: `order.refund` version 1 (ADR-080), an online refund command on the command channel. `@tallyui/core` adds the envelope and payload types, `refundPayloadErrors` (the strict shape check), the `order.refund` type in `validateBatch`, an optional `orderRefund` list in `precheckCommand`, the `refund` result in `parseCommandResult`, the refusal codes (`nothing_to_refund`, `quantity_exceeds`, `amount_mismatch`, `order_state`, `not_till_order`, `forbidden`, `no_open_session`), the `orderRefund` capability from `/tally/v1/info`, and refunds in `deriveSessionFigures`: a session's expected figures fall by the refunds it made, and it returns `refundsTotalMinor` when refunds are passed (callers that pass none get the same result as before). `@tallyui/pos` adds `sendOrderRefund(transport, envelope)`, which sends one refund and never queues it. `@tallyui/connector-vendure`'s `getVendureOrder` also reads each line's placed quantity and prorated prices and each refund's amounts and metadata, and `vendureRefundable(order)` computes what is left to refund.
- a3dc028: The order builder can apply WooCommerce coupons (#501, ADR-077 step d1). `createOrderBuilder` takes an optional `couponContext` (the coupon configs, product categories and the store's sequential-discount setting), and `setCoupons(codes)` replays the codes through the ported engine on a WooCommerce tax context. The order then carries `coupons` (`{ code, discountMinor, discountTaxMinor }`), its lines and totals are the post-coupon figures, and `display.coupons` shows one row per coupon while the display lines keep their pre-coupon amounts. Codes are trimmed, lower-cased and deduplicated. An unknown code, or one that excludes sale items, throws a `RangeError`. Other tax contexts ignore the codes. Drafts do not save coupons yet (step d2), and `useSale` does not expose them yet (step d3). `@tallyui/core` also exports `woocommerceCoupons.calculateOrderTotals` and `StoreSettings.calcDiscountsSequentially`, which the WooCommerce connector now reads from the store's settings.
- 2a79339: The till keeps a record of its refunds (ADR-080 amendment 1). `posRefundSchema` and `posRefundCollection()` define `pos_refunds`, a new local-only collection that the app adds as it adds `cash_movements`. `submitOrderRefund({ refunds, transport, envelope })` records each refund before it is sent and then stores the store's answer: `applied`, `rejected`, `unsent` (the store did not take the batch) or still `pending` (the answer is unknown, so resend the same envelope). The session's applied refunds now lower its expected figures. `deriveExpected` and `writeClosure` take optional `refunds`. The closure then fills `period_refunds_total_minor`, each method's `refunds_minor` and `refund_count`, and lists `refund_ids` and `pending_refund_ids`. `useRegisterSession` takes an optional `refunds` collection. Callers that pass no refunds get the same figures and closures as before.

#### Patch Changes

- c1bf800: Every exports entry gains a `default` condition so CommonJS and `require`-condition resolvers (Node `require`, webpack, Snack's bundler) resolve the packages; `smoke:pack` checks `require.resolve` for every entry.
- a51d0bb: `takeOverSession` now checks register contract 2 itself (#498). It takes an optional `registerContract` (the store's `capabilities.register`). Below 2, or when the contract is missing, it refuses with `RegisterTakeOverError` (`REGISTER_TAKEOVER_UNSUPPORTED`) before it reads or writes anything. A direct caller therefore can no longer queue a version 2 open for a register v1 store. `useRegisterSession` passes its contract through, so the hook behaves as before. Direct callers of `takeOverSession` must now pass `registerContract`.

### @tallyui/theme

#### Patch Changes

- c1bf800: Every exports entry gains a `default` condition so CommonJS and `require`-condition resolvers (Node `require`, webpack, Snack's bundler) resolve the packages; `smoke:pack` checks `require.resolve` for every entry.

### @tallyui/connector-woocommerce

#### Minor Changes

- a3dc028: The order builder can apply WooCommerce coupons (#501, ADR-077 step d1). `createOrderBuilder` takes an optional `couponContext` (the coupon configs, product categories and the store's sequential-discount setting), and `setCoupons(codes)` replays the codes through the ported engine on a WooCommerce tax context. The order then carries `coupons` (`{ code, discountMinor, discountTaxMinor }`), its lines and totals are the post-coupon figures, and `display.coupons` shows one row per coupon while the display lines keep their pre-coupon amounts. Codes are trimmed, lower-cased and deduplicated. An unknown code, or one that excludes sale items, throws a `RangeError`. Other tax contexts ignore the codes. Drafts do not save coupons yet (step d2), and `useSale` does not expose them yet (step d3). `@tallyui/core` also exports `woocommerceCoupons.calculateOrderTotals` and `StoreSettings.calcDiscountsSequentially`, which the WooCommerce connector now reads from the store's settings.
- 006cfb2: `ServerCapabilities` gains an optional `coupons` (#501, ADR-077 ruling R3). Absent means the store does not accept an order's coupons. The WooCommerce connector's `readWooCapabilities` now also reads `GET wcpos/v2/site`. `coupons` is true only when its `wcpos_version` is woocommerce-pos 1.9.0 or later; every unclear or failed read gives false. `orderCreate` is decided as before. Nothing reads `coupons` yet.
- 19093c2: The WooCommerce connector now has a `coupons` collection (#500, ADR-077 amendment 2). Its only source is a catalogue reconcile over the store's published coupons. Each coupon's fingerprint is `date_modified_gmt|usage_count|used_by`, because using a coupon does not move its modified time. A coupon whose fingerprint differs is refetched by id. A draft arrives deleted. A coupon that is trashed or no longer published is removed only after the store confirms it by id. `TallyConnector.reconcile` gains an optional `coupons` key. A connector without it has no coupons. Nothing reads the collection yet, and no app starts the coupons reconcile yet.

#### Patch Changes

- c1bf800: Every exports entry gains a `default` condition so CommonJS and `require`-condition resolvers (Node `require`, webpack, Snack's bundler) resolve the packages; `smoke:pack` checks `require.resolve` for every entry.

### @tallyui/connector-medusa

#### Patch Changes

- c1bf800: Every exports entry gains a `default` condition so CommonJS and `require`-condition resolvers (Node `require`, webpack, Snack's bundler) resolve the packages; `smoke:pack` checks `require.resolve` for every entry.

### @tallyui/connector-shopify

#### Patch Changes

- c1bf800: Every exports entry gains a `default` condition so CommonJS and `require`-condition resolvers (Node `require`, webpack, Snack's bundler) resolve the packages; `smoke:pack` checks `require.resolve` for every entry.

### @tallyui/connector-vendure

#### Minor Changes

- 3e2245a: `order.refund` version 1 (ADR-080), an online refund command on the command channel. `@tallyui/core` adds the envelope and payload types, `refundPayloadErrors` (the strict shape check), the `order.refund` type in `validateBatch`, an optional `orderRefund` list in `precheckCommand`, the `refund` result in `parseCommandResult`, the refusal codes (`nothing_to_refund`, `quantity_exceeds`, `amount_mismatch`, `order_state`, `not_till_order`, `forbidden`, `no_open_session`), the `orderRefund` capability from `/tally/v1/info`, and refunds in `deriveSessionFigures`: a session's expected figures fall by the refunds it made, and it returns `refundsTotalMinor` when refunds are passed (callers that pass none get the same result as before). `@tallyui/pos` adds `sendOrderRefund(transport, envelope)`, which sends one refund and never queues it. `@tallyui/connector-vendure`'s `getVendureOrder` also reads each line's placed quantity and prorated prices and each refund's amounts and metadata, and `vendureRefundable(order)` computes what is left to refund.
- 0b8a4da: Order history for Vendure (ADR-079). `listVendureOrders(context, options)` reads the channel's placed orders, newest first and paged, through the Admin API. It filters on the server by placed date, state, register (`tallyRegisterId`), cashier (`tallyCashierRef`), customer, and a search on order code or customer last name. `getVendureOrder(context, id)` reads one order with its lines, totals, tax summary, payments and refunds. Both return Vendure's own fields unmapped, need `ReadOrder` (and `ReadCustomer` for the customer filter), and write nothing locally. Pass `tallyFields: false` on a server without the vendurepos plugin.

#### Patch Changes

- c1bf800: Every exports entry gains a `default` condition so CommonJS and `require`-condition resolvers (Node `require`, webpack, Snack's bundler) resolve the packages; `smoke:pack` checks `require.resolve` for every entry.

## 3.8.0

### @tallyui/core

#### Minor Changes

- 6158b2a: Variation attributes through the product traits (#494). Core adds `VariantSummary.options` (the variant's value in each option group, keyed by group name; a group the variant accepts any value of has no key), the `VariantOptionGroup` type and the optional `ProductTraits.getVariantOptions` (the option groups in the backend's display order). The WooCommerce connector implements both: groups are the parent's attributes with `variation: true`, by `position`, with an `id` only for global attributes; replication now keeps each variation attribute's numeric `id`. Additive: no schema change, and variations stored before this release gain the id when they are next pulled.

### @tallyui/pos

#### Minor Changes

- c69c6e2: Register v2, the till's half (ADR-078 step 3b, #371). These are additive APIs; the sheets come in a later release.

  - **v2 open.** When the store advertises register contract 2 (`capabilities.register >= 2` on `useRegisterSession`), the till sends `register.session.open` as version 2, carrying the till's `deviceName` (trimmed, 1 to 64 characters). `reconcileRegisterCommands` takes `registerContract` and `deviceName`.
  - **Conflict.** A session whose open the store refused with `register_session_already_open` becomes `conflict`. It stays current for its register, so the sheet can show, but it takes no sale. Opening on that register is refused with `RegisterSessionConflictError`.
  - **Take over and Choose another register.**
    - `useRegisterSession` exposes `conflict` (the refusal's `sessionId`, `openedAt`, `openedBy` and `deviceName`, or `takingOver: true` while the take-over is in flight), plus `actions.takeOver()` and `actions.chooseAnotherRegister()`.
    - The standalone functions are `takeOverSession` and `abandonSession`.
    - Take over on a register v1 store is refused with `RegisterTakeOverError` (`REGISTER_TAKEOVER_UNSUPPORTED`). Choose another register while a take-over is in flight is refused with `REGISTER_TAKEOVER_PENDING`.
  - **`abandoned`.** Choose another register moves the session to `abandoned`, a terminal local state: it is never current or sellable, never sent, and it does not block opening. Its unsent commands are rejected locally with `register_session_abandoned`.
  - **Answers are derived from stored rows.** `adoptRegisterResults` derives resume, conflict, superseded and the way out of conflict from the stored command rows. It runs at start and on every change to the register's command rows, and `useRegisterSession` runs it for you.
  - **Outbox.** `createRegisterOutbox` and `useRegisterOutbox` take an optional `sessions` (the till's `register_sessions`). With it, an open refused as already open stops blocking its register's ledger once its session is abandoned. Without it, that refusal always blocks, so **apps should pass `sessions`**: otherwise a till that chose another register and then reopens the same one never sends again.

  **One-way storage note.** A till that rolls back past this release reads a stored `abandoned` session as "needs upgrade" (`RegisterNeedsUpgradeError`) on that register. That is accepted (ADR-078 decision 9).

### @tallyui/connector-woocommerce

#### Minor Changes

- 6158b2a: Variation attributes through the product traits (#494). Core adds `VariantSummary.options` (the variant's value in each option group, keyed by group name; a group the variant accepts any value of has no key), the `VariantOptionGroup` type and the optional `ProductTraits.getVariantOptions` (the option groups in the backend's display order). The WooCommerce connector implements both: groups are the parent's attributes with `variation: true`, by `position`, with an `id` only for global attributes; replication now keeps each variation attribute's numeric `id`. Additive: no schema change, and variations stored before this release gain the id when they are next pulled.

## 3.7.1

### @tallyui/primitives

#### Patch Changes

- 9b084a1: The Slider now moves. Pressing or dragging on it sets the value from the pointer, snapped to `step` and clamped to `min`..`max`. With the thumb focused, Arrow keys move one step, Page Up/Down move ten steps, and Home/End go to `min`/`max`. The Range's width and the Thumb's position follow the value: the primitive gives `Range` a default `width` and `Thumb` a default `position: absolute; left`, and a caller's own style still wins. The slider context gains `percent`. `disabled` ignores the pointer and the keys, and the styled `Slider` dims when disabled. (#491)

### @tallyui/pos

#### Minor Changes

- f18cc1e: A register session whose stored status this build does not know (written by a later build, then the app rolled back) now makes the register "needs upgrade" instead of counting as open. `useRegisterSession` reports `needsUpgrade: true` and no current or sellable session. Opening, counting, closing, stamping a sale and recording cash on it refuse with `RegisterNeedsUpgradeError` (code `REGISTER_NEEDS_UPGRADE`), whose message can be shown to the cashier. Reconcile never sends such a status. `isKnownSessionStatus` is exported for apps that read session rows themselves.
- 0a90e64: `register_sessions` moves to schema version 1 (ADR-078, #371). The stored `status` is an open string, so later local states need no migration; today's states add `conflict` and `superseded`. Rows gain `server_session_id`, the store session a local id was resumed as, which the migration sets to `null` on every older row.

  **Upgrade notes**

  - **Apps must switch.** Open the collection with `await addRegisterSessionCollection(db)`, which migrates a version-0 collection safely and keeps the register document. `registerSessionCollection()` now throws at start with an error naming `addRegisterSessionCollection`, because adding the collection with `addCollections` would run RxDB's own migration, which can lose sessions.
  - **Storage is one-way.** Once a till has opened this version, `register_sessions` is at schema version 1. An older build opens it without an error but shows no sessions, so the till looks closed until it is upgraded again. Nothing is deleted: the next upgrade recovers every session. Never roll an app back across this version, and never open a second session while it hides the first. See ADR-069 and ADR-078 in `docs/DECISIONS.md`.

## 3.5.0

### @tallyui/core

#### Minor Changes

- a5b432e: Products report flat categories with string ids through the new optional `getCategories` trait (WooCommerce and Medusa categories, Vendure collections, Shopify product type). `@tallyui/pos` adds `productCategories`, `listCategories` and `inCategory`, which fall back to `getCategoryNames` for connectors without the trait.

### @tallyui/components

#### Minor Changes

- f93720a: `Catalogue` gains an opt-in category nav (`showCategoryNav`, optional `categories`). "All products" comes first, then the categories of the listed products. The choice is kept in the view state as `categoryId` (new in `CatalogueViewState`, with the `setCategory` action). Visual change: `CategoryNav` chips are now rectangular (`rounded-md`) instead of pill-shaped (`rounded-full`), in every app that renders it.
  `CategoryNav` chips are also exposed as radios (`role="radio"`, `aria-checked`) in a radiogroup labelled "Categories".
- 4579599: `Catalogue` can switch between grid and table (`showViewToggle`), take a grid column count, and keep a view state the app controls or persists (`viewState`, `defaultViewState`, `loadViewState`, `saveViewState`, `onStateChange`, `items`, `onQueryChange`); new `ViewToggle` primitive and pure `catalogueViewReducer` / `normalizeCatalogueViewState` / `resolveGridColumns`. Without the new props the Catalogue is unchanged.
- 6b2277c: Add `ProductTable` (a virtualised product table with default trait columns and header sort) and the pure `sortProducts` / `productSortValue` helpers.

## 3.3.0

### @tallyui/core

#### Minor Changes

- 820b8c2: `order.create` version 5 (ADR-075): fees, shipping and custom lines in the command types, with `precheckCommand` and the payload, bounds and fiscal-figure checks; v5 fields are refused below version 5.
- 585af9b: `woocommerceTax`: WooCommerce tax primitives ported from WCPOS's order-math (MIT): `calculateTaxes` (compound, priority order, PHP rounding), `filterTaxRates` (address matching), and the rounding and tax-class helpers, for the WooCommerce connector (ADR-076).
- 71be352: A `woocommerce` tax strategy (ADR-076): several rates per class with priority and compound tax, and WooCommerce's per-rate per-line rounding (or at subtotal), through `StoreSettings.taxRates` and `TaxContext.getTaxRates`; other strategies are unchanged.

#### Patch Changes

- fcace3b: `ServerCapabilities.lineTax` and `parseLineTax`: a store's `/tally/v1/info` says whether it accepts tax-free and tax-classed fees, shipping and custom lines (order.create 5).
- 9f7cffe: A WooCommerce order can carry several payments when the store's plugin records the list
  (`order_payments_list`): the primary tender names the payment method and `_woocommerce_pos_payments` keeps all of
  them. `ServerCapabilities.multiplePayments` tells the app whether to offer split tender. The connector also reports `lineTax: { none: true, classes: true }` itself, since WCPOS has no `/tally/v1/info`.
- c7aa412: Woo products carry `tax_class` and `tax_status` (schema version 2: a till resyncs its products once) through the `getTaxClass`/`getTaxStatus` traits. Core adds the optional `getTaxStatus` trait, which the cart and `addProduct` pass to the line.
- 611ce1c: The connector now reads the store's tax settings and capabilities (the WooCommerce tax strategy, ADR-076); `parseTaxRounding` accepts the `woocommerce` granularity.

### @tallyui/pos

#### Minor Changes

- f604f87: Orders with fees, shipping or custom lines can be completed and sent as `order.create` version 5 to a store that
  accepts it (others refuse at completion, naming the plugin upgrade). `pos_orders` moves to version 8, which also
  records the woocommerce tax rounding (ADR-075, ADR-076). **Version 8 is one-way: a till that opens this release
  can't go back.**
- 71be352: A `woocommerce` tax strategy (ADR-076): several rates per class with priority and compound tax, and WooCommerce's per-rate per-line rounding (or at subtotal), through `StoreSettings.taxRates` and `TaxContext.getTaxRates`; other strategies are unchanged.

#### Patch Changes

- db9d97a: `buildClosureDocument`'s `has_sales` is true only when the period had a sale or refund (it was true for every closure, because the period totals are always strings such as "0.00").
- cec1088: The WooCommerce transport pushes tax-inclusive lines with the till's 6dp ex-tax net, as WooCommerce rebuilds a line's tax from it (ADR-076); a discounted tax-inclusive line is still refused. `CommandTransport.send` takes an optional, local-only `OrderTransportContext`.
- c7aa412: Woo products carry `tax_class` and `tax_status` (schema version 2: a till resyncs its products once) through the `getTaxClass`/`getTaxStatus` traits. Core adds the optional `getTaxStatus` trait, which the cart and `addProduct` pass to the line.

### @tallyui/connector-woocommerce

#### Patch Changes

- f604f87: Orders with fees, shipping or custom lines can be completed and sent as `order.create` version 5 to a store that
  accepts it (others refuse at completion, naming the plugin upgrade). `pos_orders` moves to version 8, which also
  records the woocommerce tax rounding (ADR-075, ADR-076). **Version 8 is one-way: a till that opens this release
  can't go back.**
- cec1088: The WooCommerce transport pushes tax-inclusive lines with the till's 6dp ex-tax net, as WooCommerce rebuilds a line's tax from it (ADR-076); a discounted tax-inclusive line is still refused. `CommandTransport.send` takes an optional, local-only `OrderTransportContext`.
- 9f7cffe: A WooCommerce order can carry several payments when the store's plugin records the list
  (`order_payments_list`): the primary tender names the payment method and `_woocommerce_pos_payments` keeps all of
  them. `ServerCapabilities.multiplePayments` tells the app whether to offer split tender. The connector also reports `lineTax: { none: true, classes: true }` itself, since WCPOS has no `/tally/v1/info`.
- c7aa412: Woo products carry `tax_class` and `tax_status` (schema version 2: a till resyncs its products once) through the `getTaxClass`/`getTaxStatus` traits. Core adds the optional `getTaxStatus` trait, which the cart and `addProduct` pass to the line.
- 611ce1c: The connector now reads the store's tax settings and capabilities (the WooCommerce tax strategy, ADR-076); `parseTaxRounding` accepts the `woocommerce` granularity.

## 3.0.0

### @tallyui/core

#### Major Changes

- 668f71f: Breaking: `precheckCommand` now takes the server's own supported versions as a required second argument, `{ orderCreate, register }`, and checks against them; its `unsupported_version` message and `data` name the server's list, not core's (#297). Plugins MUST pass their supported versions: `precheckCommand(envelope, { orderCreate: [1, 2, 3], register: [1] })`; an empty list throws a `TypeError`. This is part of 3.0.0's breaking changes and adds no extra major. `SUPPORTED_ORDER_CREATE_VERSIONS` and `SUPPORTED_REGISTER_VERSIONS` stay exported as the till's capability (what `toOrderCreateEnvelope` can produce), not what a server supports.

#### Minor Changes

- 4de75c2: A batch over 50 commands is answered `413` with `{ code: 'batch_too_large', maxCommands: 50, message: 'At most 50 commands are allowed' }`, never `400` (ADR-038, Front desk ruling 18): `@tallyui/core/server`'s `validateBatch` returns that `body` on its `413` failure so plugins send it as is. `@tallyui/core/server` now also exports `MAX_COMMANDS_PER_BATCH`, and `@tallyui/core` and `@tallyui/core/server` export the `BatchTooLargeBody` type.
- 894b6ae: **One catalogue reconcile runner** replaces the id and fingerprint reconcile runners (#248, part A). `startIdReconcile` and `startFingerprintReconcile` remain as thin wrappers with their options and results.

  - **`startCatalogueReconcile` (new)** compares the backend's product listing with the till in one pass and hands what differs to the collection's pull. It:
    - stays within a request budget (30 a minute by default) instead of a page cap, so large catalogues never truncate;
    - keeps its daily gate and a resume cursor in RxDB local documents, so it does not run on every start, and it resumes after an interruption;
    - deletes only in an uninterrupted pass, and only what the connector confirms gone. The mass-delete brake is checked on the candidates _before_ the connector is asked, and the connector is asked in chunks (`confirmChunk`, default 100), each within the budget;
    - stops or skips by `errorKind`;
    - logs what it did through an optional `log` callback.
  - **Behaviour changes for the existing runners:**
    - there is no pass 5 s after every start: the daily gate is checked at the start delay and then hourly;
    - `maxPages` is ignored;
    - requests are paced by the budget;
    - differences are refetched page by page;
    - apps that run more than one runner on the same collection pass a distinct `stateId`.
  - **`createReconcileFeed`:**
    - it takes an optional `key` to match fetched documents by the local primary key, so it works where `doc.id` is not the primary key; `fetchByIds` then receives the queued entries;
    - a `tombstone` entry is deleted without a fetch;
    - a fetched document keeps a `_deleted` the connector set.
  - **`@tallyui/core`** adds `CatalogueReconcileAdapter` and `TallyConnector.reconcile.catalogue`.
  - **Connector collections** enable RxDB local documents.

- 04905ef: `Catalogue` now applies the provider's reconciled stock overlay itself (tiles, search and the variant chooser agree), and `useStockOverlaid` and `useStockOverlayAsOf` are new.
- faa7cda: Expose ConnectorUnauthorizedError for expired or rejected stored credentials in Vendure and Medusa requests.
- 9f34416: `@tallyui/core/server` adds the batch envelope check, the per-command version pre-check, the supported versions, `totalWarnings` and `parseCommandResult` (throwing `CommandResultError`), byte-identical to the medusapos plugin's.
- fb57e1d: `@tallyui/core/server` adds the register payload check (`registerPayloadErrors`), the expected-cash derivation (`deriveSessionFigures`, `deriveVariance`) and the register conflict codes (`RegisterConflictCode`, `RegisterOutcome`), byte-identical to the medusapos plugin's.
- 898e98b: Add `@tallyui/core/server`, the plugins' shared contract: money, fingerprint, the `order.create` payload shape and fiscal figures, byte-identical to the medusapos plugin's.
- 75c5dce: `@tallyui/core/server`'s envelope types now admit register commands and any validated version, and it exports `BatchOutcome` with `inProgressOutcome` and `transientOutcome`. It also exports `CommandRejectionCode` and `platformErrorResult` (a new `platform_error` code).
- ba63f04: `CommandWarning` gains `{ code: 'customer_ignored'; customerId: string }` (#266): a sale whose `customerId` doesn't resolve is kept as a guest sale. `knownWarnings` keeps it and `parseCommandResult` accepts it when `customerId` is 1 to 64 characters; the orders list renders it.
- 0d04d13: Add neutral Customer, CustomerInput and CustomerServiceError exports and optional online-only customer search, create and get connector methods.

  Implement customer search, create and get for Medusa's admin-user connector.
